Motivation: 现有的图正则化多任务学习框架通常假设任务间依赖关系（图结构）已知，但在实际分布式场景中任务间依赖往往未知，需要直接从分布式数据中学习。因此本文旨在消除这一先验假设，实现任务关系未知情形下的去中心化多任务学习。

Method: 提出去中心化两阶段策略：第一阶段利用噪声非协作随机梯度迭代估计广义图拉普拉斯，其设计受高斯马尔可夫随机场先验启发，通过去中心化极大似然估计求解；第二阶段利用学习到的任务图驱动协作多任务扩散学习。理论上量化了图拉普拉斯估计误差及其向多任务扩散递归稳态性能的传播，并引入拓扑敏感性指标刻画网络异质性的影响。

Result: 仿真结果验证了理论分析，表明基于学习任务图启用的协作显著优于非协作学习；当估计步长足够小时，性能逼近已知真实图的基线。

Conclusion: 本文在未知任务关系下实现了去中心化多任务学习，通过先估计图再协作的两阶段框架将非协作学习转化为协作学习，并建立了从图估计误差到稳态性能的完整理论链条，为网络异质环境下任务图自学习提供了理论依据与算法支撑。

Abstract: This paper investigates decentralized multitask learning over networks when the underlying task relationships are unknown. While existing graph-regularized multitask frameworks typically assume a known structure, practical settings often require learning inter-task dependencies directly from distributed data. We propose a decentralized two-phase strategy that first estimates a generalized graph Laplacian from noisy non-cooperative stochastic gradient iterates, and subsequently exploits the learned graph to enable cooperative multitask diffusion learning. This framework is motivated by a Gaussian Markov random field prior, which gives rise to a decentralized maximum likelihood estimator for the graph Laplacian. The analysis quantifies the Laplacian estimation error and its propagation to the steady-state performance of the multitask diffusion recursion, and introduces a topology sensitivity index to capture the effect of network heterogeneity. Simulation results corroborate the theoretical findings and demonstrate that cooperation enabled by the learned task graph significantly improves performance over non-cooperative learning, while approaching the true-graph baseline when the estimation stepsize is sufficiently small.

</details>


### [264] [Benchmarking_Fast_Domain_Adaptation_for_Unsupervised_Speech_Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26992)
*Robin San Roman,Manel Khentout,Tu Anh Nguyen,Paul Michel,Yossi Adi,Emmanuel Dupoux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ABX-Accent基准，基于AESRC数据集覆盖10种英语口音，每种口音提供不足10小时的无标签训练数据，并改编了Zero Resources Challenge的ABX评估指标。同时提出一种自适应域归一化方法，对预训练CPC模型进行口音域的微调，在跨说话人ABX分数上平均取得23.6%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管表征学习在预训练和自监督语音建模中表现良好，但对其如何处理域外语音以及如何在少样本场景下适应新领域知之甚少。口音语音尤为关键，因为口音呈现长尾分布，与标准口音差异较大，现有方法难以有效覆盖。

Method: 1) 构建ABX-Accent基准：基于AESRC数据集，覆盖10种英语口音，每种口音包含小于10小时的无标签训练集，并将Zero Resources Challenge的ABX评估指标适配到每种口音。2) 提出自适应域归一化方法，在口音上微调预训练的Contrastive Predictive Coding (CPC)模型，该方法先在LibriSpeech的男/女划分上开发验证，再应用于新基准。

Result: 在ABX-Accent基准上，所提自适应域归一化方法相比未适应的模型，在跨说话人ABX分数上平均取得23.6%的相对改进。数据和指标将在论文接收后开源。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了口音语音少样本自监督适应研究的空白，提供了一个可复现的基准与基线方法，验证了利用少量无标签口音数据自适应微调预训练表征模型的有效性，为后续口音鲁棒语音建模研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Representation learning has attracted great atten- tion and managed to reach good performances as a pretraining method for downstream tasks or as a first step towards unsu- pervised speech modeling. Yet, little is known about how such methods deal with out-of-domain speech and how could they be adapted in a few shot to new domains. This is important especially for accented speech where one observes a long tail of accents that diverge from the standard ones. We introduce ABX- Accent, a benchmark based on the AESRC dataset that features 10 different accents of English. It includes a small (< 10 hours) unlabelled training set in each of the accents and adaptations of the Zero Resources Challenge ABX evaluation metrics to each of the accents. We illustrate this benchmark with a baseline model that uses adaptive domain normalization to fine tune a pretrained Contrastive Predictive Coding model on the accents. This method is first developed on LibriSpeech using a male/female split. When applied to the new benchmark, the proposed method yields a relative improvement of 23.6% on across-speaker ABX scores on average compared to non adapted models. The data and metrics will be open sourced upon paper acceptance

</details>


### [265] [Disentangling Optimization Scale from Preference Scale in DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27032)
*Ivan Kruzhilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出直接偏好优化（DPO）中的系数β实际上纠缠了两种截然不同的作用：既控制有效逆偏好噪声尺度，又同时重新缩放优化动力学（等效步长）。这种纠缠导致固定学习率下策略偏离量随β呈非单调变化，且不同β下的DPO损失值不可比，增加了超参数敏感性和学习率调度的复杂度。作者提出一种与DPO在β>0时argmin等价的centered-softplus重表述，使两种效应显式且可独立调节，并允许β→0的连续端点退化为线性偏好间隔目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO是广泛使用的语言模型对齐目标，其系数β通常被解释为控制对参考策略的KL约束。但作者发现β实际上纠缠了两个独立的角色：有效逆偏好噪声尺度与优化动力学的重缩放（即与有效步长耦合）。这一纠缠导致固定学习率下策略偏离量在β上非单调（小β时出现消失的"死区"、中间值达到峰值、更大β又下降），且不同β下的DPO损失曲线即使几乎相同，KL散度也可能相差数倍。这模糊了β的作用、增加了对超参数选择的敏感性，并使学习率调度复杂化。

Method: 作者提出一种centered-softplus重表述，该目标在β>0时与标准DPO具有相同的argmin（最优解等价），但将逆偏好噪声尺度与学习率效应显式分离并使之可独立调节。该归一化的centered-softplus目标还允许β→0的连续端点，此时退化为线性偏好间隔（linear preference-margin）目标。

Result: 理论分析表明β纠缠两个独立机制导致策略偏离量随β非单调变化（死区—峰值—下降），并证明标准DPO损失在跨β时不可比。提出的centered-softplus重表述在保持最优解不变的前提下解耦这两种效应，实现独立调节，并给出β→0的一致性退化端点。

Conclusion: 标准DPO中β的KL约束解释掩盖了其双重视角（偏好噪声尺度和优化动力学缩放）的纠缠，这损害了可解释性和训练稳定性。通过centered-softplus重表述将两者显式解耦，可获得更可控、更易调参且语义清晰的偏好优化目标，同时保持与DPO的解等价性。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) is a widely used objective for aligning language models from preference data, with the coefficient $β$ commonly interpreted as controlling the KL constraint to a reference policy. We show that $β$ entangles two distinct roles: it governs the effective inverse preference-noise scale and simultaneously rescales the optimization dynamics, coupling this scale with the effective step size. As a consequence, at a fixed learning rate the achieved policy deviation is non-monotone in $β$: it vanishes in a dead zone at small $β$, reaches a peak at an intermediate value, and decreases again for larger $β$. Moreover, standard DPO loss values are not comparable across $β$: runs with nearly identical loss curves can differ several-fold in KL divergence from the reference model. This entanglement obscures the role of $β$, increases sensitivity to hyperparameter choices, and complicates learning-rate scheduling. We propose a centered-softplus reformulation that is argmin-equivalent to DPO for $β>0$, while making the inverse preference-noise-scale and learning-rate effects explicit and independently tunable. The normalized centered-softplus objective also admits a continuous $β\to0$ endpoint that reduces to a linear preference-margin objective.

</details>


### [266] [Performance Foundations of Parallel & Distributed Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27046)
*Maciej Besta,Leonard Schmidt,Lara Nonino,Robert Gerstenberger,Pierre Pang,Patrik Okanovic,Ales Kubicek,Tiancheng Chen,Baraq Lipshitz,Torsten Hoefler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统化梳理了基于强化学习的推理语言模型（RLM）训练范式，提供了面向计算开销的分析，涵盖PPO、GRPO及其变体等主流后训练算法框架，并建立了针对多模型RLM训练的并行策略分类体系（包括传统并行技术与新兴的分离部署、阶段融合、混合并行、异步执行等），最后基于work-depth并行计算模型给出可扩展、高效、低成本RLM训练的实用指南与开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RLVR等RL风格后训练范式（DeepSeek-R1、o3、Kimi k1.5等）能显著提升LLM的链式推理、长程规划与自我纠错能力，但其计算开销巨大：最先进的RLM训练需数百万GPU小时并依赖紧密耦合的多模型流水线，远超传统监督式LLM训练，使RLM训练不仅是算法问题，更是并行与分布式系统问题，因此亟需系统化、计算中心化的分析与指南。

Method: 作者首先从系统角度系统化RL-for-LLM范式，并对PPO、GRPO及其变体等主流后训练算法框架进行以计算为中心的分析；其次构建RL-for-LLM的模型内与模型间并行策略分类体系，涵盖数据、张量、流水线、序列、上下文、专家并行等传统技术，以及分离式放置、阶段融合、混合并行、异步执行等针对多模型RLM训练的新型并行与优化技术；最后借助并行计算的work-depth模型使该分类体系及其洞察更严格、更具可移植性，并据此分析现有RLM框架。

Result: 该工作产出了（1）面向RLM后训练的算法框架计算分析、（2）涵盖传统与新型并行策略的完整分类体系及基于work-depth模型的严格化表述、（3）对现有RLM框架的分析以及兼顾高性能、可扩展性与成本效益的实用指南和开放研究方向。

Conclusion: 作者通过系统化的分类与work-depth形式化分析，论证了将RLM训练视为并行与分布式系统问题的必要性，并总结出构建可扩展、快速且成本效益高的RLM的实践准则，同时指出该领域仍有待探索的开放研究方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) and other RL-style post-training paradigms have been used for aligning large language models (LLMs) with reasoning standards. The resulting recent Reasoning Language Models (RLMs) such as DeepSeek-R1, o3, and Kimi k1.5 show that such RL-style post-training ("RL-for-LLMs") can substantially improve chain-of-thought reasoning, long-horizon planning, and self-correction. However, the computational footprint of these systems is massive: state-of-the-art RLM training requires millions of GPU-hours and tightly coupled multi-model pipelines that stress modern hardware far beyond classical supervised LLM training. This makes RLM training as much a parallel and distributed systems problem as an algorithmic one. In this work, to facilitate developing RLMs that are simultaneously high-performance, scalable, and cost-effective, we first systematize the RL-for-LLM paradigm and provide a compute-centric analysis of prominent post-training algorithmic frameworks: Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), as well as their variants. Second, we develop a taxonomy of intra- and inter-model parallelism strategies for RL-for-LLMs, covering both traditional techniques (data, tensor, pipeline, sequence, context, and expert parallelism) as well as novel forms of parallelism and optimization techniques for multi-model RLM training, for example disaggregated placement, stage fusion, hybrid parallelism, and asynchronous execution. We harness the work-depth model of parallel computing to make our taxonomy and its insights rigorous and portable. Finally, we analyze existing RLM frameworks and we distill practical guidelines and outline open research directions for building scalable, fast, and cost-effective RLMs.

</details>


### [267] [Soft Active Electromyography Interface for Machine Learning-Enabled Silent Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27048)
*Yuta Kurotaki,Shusuke Yamakoshi,Reitaro Yoshida,Yutaka Isoda,Tamami Takano,Yuji Isano,Yusuke Miyake,Kentaro Kuribayashi,Hiroki Ota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种佩戴于手部的软性主动肌电（EMG）界面，通过在指尖集成电极（用时靠近嘴唇）并结合机器学习实现词级无声语音识别，在30词词汇分类上达到97.2%的平均准确率，并在真实环境中成功验证了无人机控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无声语音识别（SSR）方法受限于需要持续佩戴在面部、存在隐私顾虑以及信号采集不稳定等问题，因此需要一种更灵活、安全且采集可靠的替代方案。

Method: 设计了一种软性、主动式EMG可穿戴界面：佩戴于手部，指尖电极可在需要时移至唇旁采集EMG信号；器件集成液态金属互连、透明柔性印刷电路（FPC）电极和弹性体封装，以保证手指运动时的高机械稳定性；并采用深度神经网络对采集到的稳定信号进行词级分类。

Result: 在3名受试者上对30词词汇进行分类，平均准确率达97.2% ± 1.3%，展现出稳健的语言判别能力；此外，在嘈杂且注重隐私的环境中，实时无人机控制实验验证了该方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该研究凸显了软性可穿戴EMG系统作为安全、直观的人机交互界面的潜力，为无声语音识别提供了一种免面部佩戴、隐私友好且信号稳定的新途径。

Abstract: Silent speech recognition (SSR) provides an alternative communication pathway in the absence of audible speech. However, conventional approaches are limited by the need for constant facial attachment, privacy concerns, and unstable signal acquisition. Here, we propose a soft, active electromyography (EMG) interface that enables word-level SSR using machine learning. Worn on the hand, the device uses a fingertip electrode that can be positioned near the lips to acquire EMG signals only when needed. The interface integrates liquid metal (LM) interconnects, transparent flexible printed circuit (FPC) electrodes, and elastomer encapsulation to ensure high mechanical stability during finger motion. A deep neural network trained on these stable signals achieved a mean accuracy of 97.2 $\pm$ 1.3% across three subjects in classifying a 30-word vocabulary, demonstrating robust linguistic discrimination. Furthermore, real-time drone control validates the practicality of this approach in noisy and privacy-sensitive environments where conventional voice recognition fails. This study highlights the potential of soft, wearable EMG systems as secure and intuitive human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [268] [Unifying Detection and Adaptation in Task-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27070)
*Dezheng Han,Anbang Zhang,Zhihao Zhu,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FiUni（Fisher引导的统一框架），利用预训练模型Fisher信息矩阵（FIM）的K-FAC近似主子空间之间的正交性来反映任务相似性，从而在无任务边界的场景下实现批量级任务检测与参数高效的持续学习。通过构造FIM衍生的冻结子空间引导LoRA，并匹配每个输入批次的Fisher主子空间与历史子空间，FiUni能够自适应地决定复用已有知识、扩展相关子空间或创建新子空间，以动态平衡知识共享与任务隔离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM持续学习方法（如参数约束、任务特定适配模块）通常依赖训练过程中显式的任务边界，这在现实中的任务无关（task-free）场景下难以适用。因此需要一种无需任务边界、能够自动检测任务归属并实现参数高效持续适应的统一框架。

Method: FiUni框架包括两个核心部分：(1) 基于预训练模型FIM的K-FAC近似，估计少量下游任务样本的主子空间，并利用主子空间之间的正交性来衡量任务相似性；(2) 构造FIM衍生的冻结子空间来引导低秩适配（LoRA），同时将每个到达批次窗口的Fisher主子空间与历史子空间进行匹配，从而自适应地选择复用已有知识、扩展相关子空间或创建新子空间。

Result: 实验表明，FiUni能够有效推断隐式的批量级任务归属，并在使用更少可训练参数的情况下，取得了与先进的任务感知（task-aware）持续学习方法相当甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: FiUni通过Fisher信息引导的子空间建模，成功消除了持续学习对显式任务边界的依赖，实现了知识共享与任务隔离之间的动态平衡，为真实任务无关场景下的LLM持续适应提供了有效且参数高效的解决方案。

Abstract: To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in downstream continual learning (CL) for large language models (LLMs), existing methods typically constrain parameter updates or introduce task-specific adaptation modules. However, these methods often rely on explicit task boundaries during training, limiting their applicability to realistic task-free scenarios. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{Fi}sher-guided \textbf{uni}fied (\textbf{FiUni}) framework for batch-level task detection and parameter-efficient continual adaptation. FiUni is motivated by a key observation about the Fisher information matrix (FIM) of pre-trained models: the orthogonality among the principal subspaces of its Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) approximation, estimated from a small number of downstream task samples, can reflect the similarity between different tasks. Based on this observation, FiUni constructs FIM-derived frozen subspaces to guide low-rank adaptation (LoRA), while matching the Fisher principal subspace of each incoming batch window with historical subspaces. This enables FiUni to adaptively determine whether to reuse existing knowledge, expand a related subspace, or create a new subspace, dynamically balancing knowledge sharing and task isolation. Experiments show that FiUni can effectively infer latent batch-level task affiliations and achieve competitive performance against advanced task-aware CL methods with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [269] [Emotional Preferences as Goal-Priority Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27072)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihua Tan,Hu Fu,Guanyu Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于目标导向的情绪理论，提出了一种"涌现性情绪偏好"概念：智能体的高层目标通过强化学习自主生成对竞争性低层目标的状态依赖偏好，而非由外部预先指定。该架构由一个多目标强化学习内控制器（提供偏好条件化的目标导向行为库）和一个外层偏好生成器（通过高层目标上的强化学习学习状态到目标偏好的映射）组成。论文刻画了偏好调节诱导的策略空间，推导了以内部行为库表示误差为参数的最优性差距上界，并在自建多目标探索环境中验证了该方法在情境化优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持续性上优于固定偏好与手工偏好策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体决策中的核心问题在于：竞争性低层目标的相对优先级能否由高层目标自主生成的情绪偏好来决定，而非依赖外部预先指定。在外部环境变化和内部状态演化的条件下，情绪在调节竞争目标相对优先级方面具有重要功能作用，但目前缺乏如何在计算上实现这种偏好调节的研究。

Method: 提出"涌现性情绪偏好"概念，即高层目标自主诱导对竞争性低层目标的状态依赖偏好。具体架构包括：(1) 内控制器：多目标强化学习方法，提供偏好条件化的目标导向行为库（repertoire）；(2) 外层偏好生成器：在高层目标上进行强化学习，学习从当前状态到目标偏好的映射。论文将情绪偏好操作化为通过优化涌现的状态依赖的相对目标优先级调节，并从理论上刻画偏好调节诱导的策略空间、推导基于内部行为库表示误差的最优性差距上界。

Result: 在自建的多目标探索环境中，学习到的偏好函数展现出情境化优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持续性等性质，并在性能上优于所评估的固定偏好策略和手工偏好策略。理论上，最优性差距上界随内部行为库的表示误差趋于零；当最优策略可由可用的偏好条件化策略表示时，该差距消失。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过强化学习可以将情绪驱动的偏好调节在计算上实现为高层目标对低层目标优先级的状态依赖调控，从而让智能体在动态环境中自适应地权衡竞争性目标。该框架为自主偏好生成提供了理论基础与实证验证，其最优性差距可被行为库的表示误差所界定，为后续研究提供了可解释的性能保证。

Abstract: A core question in decision-making for agents is whether the relative priorities of competing lower-level objectives can be determined by emotional preferences autonomously generated by higher-level goals, rather than being externally prespecified. Under changing external environments and evolving internal states, emotions play an important functional role in regulating the relative priorities of competing goals. Inspired by the goal-directed theory of emotion, this paper studies how such preference regulation can be computationally realized through reinforcement learning. We first propose a conception of emergent emotional preference: a high-level goal autonomously induces state-dependent preferences over competing lower-level objectives. This conception is built upon a framework consisting of a multi-objective reinforcement learning inner controller and an outer preference generator. The inner controller provides a repertoire of preference-conditioned goal-directed behaviors, while the outer preference generator learns a mapping from the current state to objective preferences through reinforcement learning on a high-level goal. We operationalize emotional preference as a state-dependent regulation of relative goal priorities that emerges through optimization. Furthermore, we characterize the policy space induced by preference regulation and derive an upper bound on the optimality gap in terms of the representation error of the inner behavioral repertoire. We show that the gap vanishes when the optimal policy can be represented by the available preference-conditioned policies. Experiments in self-constructed multi-objective exploration environments show that the learned preference function exhibits contextual priority switching, graded trade-offs, and temporal persistence, and outperforms the evaluated fixed-preference and handcrafted-preference strategies.

</details>


### [270] [Tabular Deep Learning for Algorithmic Trading: Cross-Regime Bayesian Optimisation for Equity Signal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27076)
*Joshua Le Grice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了算法交易中股票预测模型的制度稳健性（regime robustness）。在近300只美国大盘股11年日频数据上，用贝叶斯优化在三个统计上不同的市场制度下针对交易表现进行超参数选择。结果表明制度稳健的超参数选择能带来更好的样本外泛化能力；基于排名聚合融合XGBoost与TabNet的Hybrid集成取得了年化收益51.26%、夏普比率2.44、CAPM alpha 0.423（p=0.011）且Beta接近零的业绩，说明超额收益来自选股而非市场敞口。另类数据在技术面与基本面特征之后贡献次要，且做空端贡献强于做多端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法交易市场规模已超200亿美元，信号稳健性的微小提升即可转化为可观的经济收益；然而现有股票预测模型评估在超参数选择阶段并未明确针对市场制度稳健性进行优化，导致模型在不同市场环境下泛化能力不明。

Method: 在近300只美国大盘股11年日频数据上训练五类模型，使用贝叶斯优化将超参数选择目标设定为在三个统计上不同的市场制度下的交易表现；并采用排名聚合（rank aggregation）将XGBoost与TabNet组合为Hybrid集成模型，同时评估模拟输入噪声下的退化行为与另类数据特征的边际贡献。

Result: 制度稳健的超参数选择与样本外泛化相关：测试期四个季度的信号精度均高于随机基线，组合表现随模拟噪声缓慢退化并在阈值后崩溃。无单个表格深度学习架构优于梯度提升树，但XGBoost与TabNet的Hybrid集成实现年化收益51.26%、夏普比率2.44及统计显著的CAPM alpha 0.423（p=0.011），接近零的Beta表明超额收益源于选股。另类数据在技术面与基本面之后贡献次要，做空端贡献强于做多端且因模型类别而异。

Conclusion: 结论是制度稳健的超参数选择是提升股票预测模型样本外泛化能力的关键设计原则；集成方法（尤其跨模型类别融合）优于单一架构。另类数据并非主导因素，需按方向与模型类别差异取舍。交互式应用已使结果可实现实时探索，接入选为直接落地部署后的下一步工作。

Abstract: Algorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

</details>


### [271] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有向图学习中的可训练非对称传播算子，提出了一种基于双侧锥Rayleigh框架（广义束 B_θ−λG）的学习导向方法，用于谱认证、灵敏度分析与控制。在正卦限设定下，通过可计算的下/上锥界对区分的锥水平进行后验包络，并引入光滑soft-min/max代理保留严谨单侧界及显式逼近误差，同时保持对可训练参数可微。对内点水平，右/左特征模态满足 Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C，从而得到扰动预算约束下的一阶最优图干预与自适应谱控制。实验表明该方法适用于非锥保持算子与有向学习场景：符号非对称扰动展现内点特征对向边界互补拟对的转变，对照组显示对称化会移除仅由边方向携带的预测信息；在Cora有向引文网络上，自适应重算右-左灵敏度在累计边权削减预算0.5%下将区分的谱水平降低约21.5%，且测试准确率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图学习天然产生可训练的非对称传播算子，其右/左谱结构不同，而传统谱方法通常依赖对称性、非负性或锥保持等假设。作者旨在建立一种不依赖这些假设、且可微并适用于学习流程的谱认证、灵敏度分析与控制框架，以便在可训练参数下进行可靠的理论保证与干预设计。

Method: 基于双侧锥Rayleigh框架处理广义束 [B_θ−λG]，推导可计算的下/上锥界以对区分的锥水平进行后验包络；采用光滑soft-min/max代理在保持严谨单侧界和显式逼近误差的同时保持对参数的微分性。利用内点水平的导数关系 Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C 与右/左特征模态，导出一阶最优的图支持干预策略和自适应谱控制算法。

Result: 数值实验显示方法可超越锥保持算子的限制并适用于有向学习设置；符号非对称扰动呈现从内点特征对到边界互补拟对（含非谱锥水平）的转变；对照实验表明对称化会移除仅由边方向携带的预测信息。在Cora有向引文网络上，自适应右-左灵敏度重算在累积边权削减预算0.5%下使区分的谱水平下降约21.5%，且对已训练模型与所考虑数据划分的测试准确率无影响。

Conclusion: 本文证明：即使在不假设对称性、非负性或锥保持的有向学习场景中，仍可通过锥Rayleigh框架获得严谨、可微且可计算的谱界，并借助右-左谱灵敏度实现高效的谱控制。谱水平可观的降低且预测性能不变表明该类干预是有向图学习系统可用的有效调控手段，而对称化破坏信息则凸显了非对称（方向性）谱结构的必要价值。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [272] [TRACE-CRC: Trajectory-Adaptive Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Step Channel State Information Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27124)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Mehdi Sattari,Javad Aliakbari,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE-CRC，一种面向多步 CSI 预测的轨迹感知不确定性量化方法，通过 conformal risk control 为预测的 CSI 矩阵序列构造 Frobenius 范数不确定性球，并控制至少一个未来帧未被覆盖的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于深度学习的 CSI 预测器通常只提供点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性估计；在多步预测中，目标是未来 CSI 矩阵序列，若轨迹的任何部分不可靠，波束成形或调度等下游决策可能失败。

Method: TRACE-CRC 构造围绕预测 CSI 矩阵的 Frobenius 范数不确定性球，并控制“至少一个未来帧未被覆盖”的风险；它不独立校准每个未来步，而是结合未来步相关误差画像、轨迹难度分层以及 learn-then-test (LTT) 风险控制。

Result: 经验结果表明，TRACE-CRC 能实现可靠的轨迹级覆盖率，且不确定性球显著小于保守的多步校正方法，同时避免了逐步行与自适应共形基线导致的轨迹覆盖不足。

Conclusion: TRACE-CRC 以紧凑的不确定性球为多步 CSI 预测提供了可靠的轨迹级不确定性保证，兼顾了覆盖可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of time-varying channel state information (CSI) is essential for efficient wireless communication. Each CSI frame is a matrix-valued representation of the wireless channel response, and a sequence of CSI frames forms a temporal channel trajectory. Modern deep learning-based CSI predictors, however, often provide only point predictions and lack calibrated uncertainty estimates. This limitation is particularly problematic in multi-step CSI prediction, where the target is a sequence of future CSI matrices, and downstream decisions such as beamforming or scheduling may fail if any part of the predicted trajectory is unreliable. We propose trajectory-adaptive calibration and error profiling with conformal risk control (TRACE-CRC), a method for trajectory-aware uncertainty quantification in multi-step CSI prediction. TRACE-CRC constructs Frobenius-norm uncertainty balls around predicted CSI matrices and controls the risk that at least one future frame is uncovered. Instead of calibrating each future step independently, TRACE-CRC combines future-step-dependent error profiling, trajectory difficulty stratification, and learn-then-test (LTT) risk control. Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [273] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于接收信号强度（RSS）的新型定位方法，通过少量RSS测量和概率建模，实现超轻量（38mg）、超低功耗（<180uW）移动接收器在复杂环境中的约15米精度跟踪，并扩展到约10米精度（<600uW），已用于追踪熊蜂归巢飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动生态学、物联网和机器人等领域需要定位体积过小、功耗受限而无法使用GNSS的设备。现有低功耗定位方法通常仅用RSS测量推断信号到达角（AoA），但受限于作用距离以及为获得精确AoA所需的大量RSS测量带来的功耗负担。

Method: 使用简单旋转高增益发射机（覆盖300米范围）获取极少量的RSS测量，通过概率建模推断接收器的AoA；接收器运动路径用高斯过程建模，并采用双重随机变分推断进行路径重建。

Result: 对含电源仅重38mg的接收器，可在可扩展景观范围内实现约15米精度的跟踪，功耗低于180uW；通过增加RSS测量可提升至约10米精度，功耗低于600uW。该系统被用于追踪熊蜂（Bombus terrestris）的归巢飞行。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低的重量和功耗代价实现了可扩展、中等精度的移动接收器跟踪，有望支持飞行昆虫行为研究等对微型化追踪有刚性需求的领域。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [274] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的归纳相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering）框架，通过学习训练阶段中的公共结构模式和节点特征，能够高效处理未见过的图实例，将推理时间降低多达5个数量级，同时保持近似比与最优基线相差约10%以内，并在标准传导性聚类基准上取得有竞争力的结果，还可作为可学习的池化机制增强GNN捕获层级结构信息的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关聚类（CC）是组合优化中一种自然的聚类问题，可根据成对相似度函数构建聚类且无需预先指定簇数量。然而，现有CC算法存在显著的扩展性问题，并且本质上是传导性的（transductive），即每个新问题实例都必须从头重新运行算法，无法利用先前学习的知识。

Method: 作者利用图神经网络（GNN）解决归纳相关聚类（Inductive Correlation Clustering），这是CC问题的一个新推广，用于处理未见过的图实例。该框架在训练阶段学习利用常见的结构模式和节点特征，从而以相比标准算法极小的计算开销，泛化到来自同一分布的新图上。此外，作者将该框架作为可学习的池化机制（learnable pooling）用于图分类任务。

Result: 大量实验表明：在归纳设置下，该框架可将推理时间降低多达5个数量级，同时近似比保持在最优基线解的约10%以内；在标准传导性CC基准上也取得了有竞争力的结果；作为池化层时，能够有效增强GNN捕获网络中层级结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架具有高效性与可扩展性，不仅解决了CC算法扩展性差和只能传导推理的局限，还兼具竞争力与实用价值，可作为一种高效的池化层来增强GNN捕获层级结构信息的能力，展示了其在归纳聚类与图学习中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [275] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PDPO，一种基于扩散策略的离线到在线强化学习框架，通过生成短时程动作块（action chunks）并以滚动时域方式执行来实现机器人人群导航；同时指出常见基准中智能体可通过越界绕过密集人群的评估伪影，并将边界违规视为碰撞以修正设定。实验表明PDPO在成功率上优于强基线，且消融显示动作块对修正后的有界基准尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在每个时间步只输出单一的反应式动作，难以表达多样化的短期避障策略，限制了密集、动态、多模态人机交互场景下的决策能力；此外，常见人群导航基准存在评估伪影：缺乏显式边界约束时，学习到的智能体会离开有效区域、绕过密集人群，而非真正完成导航任务。

Method: 提出Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO)：先基于避障演示离线预训练扩散策略，再用PPO在线微调，并将去噪过程视为内部决策过程。执行时策略生成五步动作块，并以滚动时域方式应用；同时引入将边界违规视为碰撞的有界新设定，以消除评估伪影。

Result: 实验表明PDPO在成功率上优于强基线；消融实验证实动作块对于修正后的有界基准尤为重要，验证了动作块和边界约束相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 将扩散策略与短时程动作块结合、并通过离线到在线训练框架，可有效提升人群导航的安全性与成功率；同时，修复基准中的越界评估伪影对公平、真实地衡量导航能力至关重要。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [276] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TraceBench，一个基于仿真的框架，用于生成受控的根因归因任务，以系统评估 LLM 智能体在时间序列异常检测与根因分析中的表现；实验发现智能体显著受益于领域上下文、主要通过数值控制台输出探索数据，且直接提交预测比生成 Python 脚本映射预测标签的表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地被应用于真实系统时间序列观测的异常检测和根因分析，但其在受控条件下的表现缺乏系统性评估，现有研究未能清晰揭示这些智能体如何完成此类任务。

Method: 构建 TraceBench 仿真框架：通过模拟物理动力系统生成受控的根因归因任务，每个任务中智能体接收模拟产生的时间序列观测，需判断系统参数是否在模拟过程中被改变以及是哪一个参数；基于三个可解释的机械系统生成任务，并在受控实验条件下系统评估四个 LLM 智能体。

Result: 实验结果揭示了智能体分析动力系统时间序列的新见解：领域上下文能显著提升智能体表现；智能体主要通过数值控制台输出而非可视化来探索数据；当要求智能体生成将每个时间序列样本映射到预测根因标签的 Python 脚本时，其表现普遍差于直接提交预测。

Conclusion: 作者公开了数据集、智能体轨迹、实验结果和排行榜（tracebench.github.io），为 LLM 智能体在时间序列根因归因任务上的受控评测提供了基准资源，并指出任务形式（脚本生成 vs 直接提交）对智能体性能有显著影响。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [277] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对随时间演化的交互网络中的同伴效应（peer effects）估计问题，提出了一种“受控对比”（controlled contrast）框架，将潜在结果按自身处理、时间聚合的同伴暴露与事后网络演化摘要进行索引，并据此开发了双重稳健估计量 DynaNet-DR，其在结果回归或倾向得分任一模型一致时均能保持一致性，并通过半合成基准与 MathOverflow 观察性数据验证了其估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当交互图随时间演化时，同伴效应的估计十分困难，因为分配前网络历史、动态同伴暴露以及分配后网络变化在因果上扮演不同角色；现有方法难以厘清这些成分，导致同伴效应无法被准确识别。

Method: 提出受控对比框架，用自身处理、时间聚合的同伴暴露和事后演化摘要为潜在结果建立索引，通过均值差异定义自身处理、同伴暴露、受控网络演化和联合受控对比（而非中介分解）；在此基础上提出 DynaNet-DR（动态网络双重稳健估计量），结合时间因子化倾向得分与归一化增强。实证实现中加入代表性评分预测、固定截断和有限样本稳定化。

Result: 在一致性、摘要充分性、序列可交换性、正性、扰动收敛和弱依赖等假定下，当结果回归或倾向得分估计器之一一致时，其规范估计量保持一致。基于固定真实时序图序列的半合成基准表明，在面向完整对比画像的方法中其估计精度更优；MathOverflow 研究作为既定假定下的观察性说明。

Conclusion: DynaNet-DR 为动态网络中的同伴效应估计提供了兼具因果清晰性与双重稳健性的可行工具，能够刻画并分别估计多种受控对比；但其基准与实证均以摘要索引的对比为目标（而非反事实边生成），结果依赖一系列识别假定，需在应用中谨慎验证。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [278] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化支持向量机（SSVM）的 Fisher 一致性：已知任务损失为满足“每个输出三元组存在公共测地点”的度量是必要但不充分条件。作者证明在坐标式 argmax 解码器下该条件不充分，并完整刻画了树度量上的 argmax 一致性，指出分歧树的失败仅出现在边界分布，而保形嵌入可能只保证校准链接存在、却无法保证指定 argmax 链接在每个代理风险最小化器上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化预测中，SSVM 的 Fisher 一致性依赖任务损失满足度量条件（每三元组有公共测地点）。但该已知必要条件是否足够、特别是对典型坐标式 argmax 解码器是否成立，此前未被严格回答，本文旨在填补这一理论空白并揭示解码器差距。

Method: 采用纯理论/组合分析方法：构造最小反例（四输出单位星、五输出的 K_{2,3} 及三维 Hamming 立方体），对所有最优性声明给出精确的原-对偶证书；对正加权树度量（输出空间为顶点集）进行完整分类，并区分边界分布与全支撑分布两种情形。

Result: (1) 公共测地条件并非坐标式 argmax 解码器一致性的充分条件；(2) 在正加权树度量上，argmax 一致性成立当且仅当树为路径，四输出是最小规模反例；(3) 树上的失败局限于边界分布，任意树在全支撑分布下保持 argmax 性质；(4) 在满足公共测地条件的度量中，五个输出即可且必要地构造全支撑反例，K_{2,3} 是最小的 K_{m,n} 族成员；(5) 三维 Hamming 立方体也存在全支撑反例。

Conclusion: 公共测地条件不足以保障 SSVM 在坐标式 argmax 解码器下的 Fisher 一致性；树度量与特定图族上的一致性已被完整刻画。反例揭示了具体的“解码器差距”：在多面体设定中，嵌入只能保证存在某个校准链接，而无法保证预设的 argmax 链接在所有代理风险最小化器上有效，从而为设计更严格的一致性条件或改进解码器提供了依据。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [279] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出以利润（而非预测精度）直接给氮肥推荐打分的方法，基于892条实测产量响应曲线构建测试基准，并扫描氮价/粮价比覆盖全部价格情景。结果显示机器学习在预测上失败、在利润上也不如标准建议，但将其作为"标准建议+阻尼修正"的混合组件时，可削减约1/4至43%的利润损失，且无需重训；同时该价格扫描还可为减排措施定价。结论是机器学习的价值在于作为标准建议的利润评分修正，而非替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冬小麦氮肥用量在季节前设定，面临未知的价格与天气；英国标准建议不随价格调整，而近期价格波动使最优施氮量移动数十公斤/公顷。机器学习虽常被提议作为解决方案，但其评价通常只看预测精度，而准确预测本身并不能让推荐施肥量变得更有利可图，因此需要直接以利润来评估氮肥建议。

Method: 核心洞察是用实测产量响应曲线上的利润损失（forgone profit）直接给氮肥建议打分。作者利用两个长期英国试验中的892条产量响应曲线构建测试基准，并扫描氮价与粮价比值以覆盖所有价格情景。在此基准上比较标准建议、机器学习模型，以及"标准建议加简单修正""标准建议加阻尼修正（混合）"等策略。

Result: 在该基准上机器学习作为预测器失败：没有任何模型能在农场容许误差内恢复最优施氮量，且基准噪声表明无人能做到；在正常价格下所有模型在利润上也输给标准建议。增益来自其他环节——模型后加一个简单修正步骤可将利润损失削减约1/4，而更好的模型和额外特征并无增益；同一冻结修正无需重训即可在第二个试验点将损失削减43%。混合"标准建议+阻尼修正"可去除偏差并削减罕见的大额损失。此外，相同的价格扫描还可为减排措施定价，其成本与当前碳价格相当。

Conclusion: 机器学习应以利润评分的方式服务于标准建议的修正（而非作为其替代品），这样既能显著降低利润损失、提升稳健性，又能在同一框架下评估减排成本。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [280] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HALO 是一个面向 IMU 人体活动识别（HAR）的领域专用基础模型，通过两阶段训练（异构感知自监督预训练 + 同义词感知软对比学习的语言对齐）同时解决传感异构性与对新活动/标签词汇的开放集泛化问题，在 10 个训练数据集、7 个留出数据集的评估中以约 35M 参数全面超越 5 个 SOTA 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 IMU 的 HAR 领域仍缺乏能在不同受试者、设备和活动间统一泛化的基础模型，其训练面临两大挑战：一是传感异构性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置差异），二是对未见活动与标签词汇的泛化能力差。

Method: 提出两阶段训练框架：阶段一采用异构感知自监督学习预训练 IMU 编码器，包括自适应池化分词（adaptive-pooling tokenization）、通道独立特征提取，以及将自然语言传感器描述注入各通道嵌入的上下文传感器条件化（contextualized sensor conditioning）；阶段二通过同义词感知软对比学习（synonym-aware soft contrastive learning）将 IMU 编码器与文本嵌入对齐，利用余弦相似度检索实现开放集识别，无需逐数据集分类器。

Result: 在 10 个公共 HAR 数据集上训练、7 个留出数据集上评估，HALO 在全部 8 项聚合指标上优于 5 个 SOTA 基线，并在基线匹配输入下仍于 4 项设置中领先 3 项；仅用约 35M 可训练参数（比最新基础模型 MOMENT 的 341.2M 少 10 倍），即在 87 个训练标签的零样本开放集准确率上提升 13.7 个百分点；但在两个分布偏移严重的额外数据集上，包括 HALO 在内的所有模型零样本能力均失效。

Conclusion: HALO 验证了"异构感知 + 语言对齐 + 开放集检索"路径能构建高效、强泛化的 IMU 基础模型，以极小参数量取得 SOTA 性能；同时揭示在严重分布偏移下零样本开放集识别仍存在根本性局限，为后续研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [281] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将注意力头重要性评分指标应用于表格数据的多头Transformer模型解释，通过在40个表格数据集上的实验验证了该指标的有效性，并发现重要注意力头散布在各层且随数据集模式变化，可据此提升Transformer的效率和冗余度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型计算量大且不透明，可通过分析其数据变换方式加以理解和优化。虽然深度Transformer已在计算机视觉和自然语言处理领域被广泛研究，但其在表格数据上的应用仍相对未被充分探索。因此需要一种可解释方法来理解表格数据中的Transformer行为。

Method: 提出一种基于重要性评分（importance-scoring）的度量指标，用于解释多Head注意力Transformer在表格数据学习中的行为。在40个多样化的表格数据集上进行实验，采用逐步移除注意力头的策略：分别按最低和最高重要性分数移除头，观察分类性能变化，并分析各层独立头的重要性分布。

Result: 在72.5%的实验样本中，逐步移除最低重要性分数注意力头时模型表现最稳健；反之，最先移除最重要注意力头会导致分类性能最大幅度下降。对六层注意力层的个体头部重要性进行细粒度分析发现，重要头部散布在各层间，无一致的分层趋势。与图像和语言领域不同，头部重要性在不同模式（schema）和特征空间的表格数据集之间差异显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的重要性评分可用于提升Transformer架构内的效率和冗余度，帮助识别并移除冗余注意力头而不显著损害性能。作者公开了衡量各注意力头重要性的源代码，便于后续研究使用与验证。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [282] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Circuit Condensation（电路压缩）方法，通过"剪枝低归因边+训练低秩适配器"的迭代后训练，使模型将特定行为集中到更小的因果图上。在四类行为、八个模型的32个设置中，压缩电路在30个设置里优于最强冻结基线，平均小8.1倍、最高达316倍，并证明压缩主要来自权重更新而非搜索本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性通过"电路"（承载行为的组件与连接）来解释模型行为，但冻结式电路发现常返回数百条边，导致电路难以检查、比较或穷举验证。作者希望获得更小的、可验证的电路，以解决可解释性研究中的可操作性瓶颈。

Method: 迭代式后训练压缩：每轮先剪除低归因的边，再通过剩余边训练一个低秩适配器以匹配原始模型输出；仅在任务性能与通用能力均保留时才保留该剪枝。通过对比实验验证权重更新的必要性（无更新的重搜索在29/32设置中产生更大电路），并用子集遍历（19个电路中有11个不可再缩减）和成对消融来检验可移除性与边间依赖。

Result: 压缩电路在30/32个设置中比最强冻结基线更小，平均小8.1倍、最多316倍；权重重训而非搜索是压缩的关键驱动。在间接宾语识别任务上，压缩仅需24个注意力头（其中17个已有文献记录的功能），而匹配的冻结电路需61个头（36个无记录）；压缩电路能追踪原模型的next-token分布并预测其错误。

Conclusion: 压缩得到的电路是已发表机制的"充分子电路"而非对它的重构，既能保留原模型行为又能预测其错误，因而比冻结发现的电路更小、更可检查、可比较、可穷举验证，为机械可解释性提供了一种更实用的电路发现范式。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [283] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在演化算子网络（LEON），将世界动作模型（WAM）中的潜在状态转移实现为基于可控Koopman生成元的算子式传播，替代以token交互为中心的Transformer式预测器，从而引入演化特定的归纳偏置，提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAM的潜在状态转移普遍采用Transformer类预测器，其归纳结构以token交互为核心，而非时间演化，导致潜在转移实现与演化任务的特性不匹配。作者主张将"转移实现"视为独立于预测表征和预测-策略耦合的架构选择加以研究。

Method: LEON在一个学习到的可观测空间中实现潜在演化，采用上下文调制的算子式传播（operator-based propagation）与加性强迫（additive forcing）两条互补路径。其设计源于受控Koopman生成元视角：将依赖于上下文的转移变化围绕共享的演化算子结构组织起来，同时保留一条补充性的加性变化路径。

Result: 在受控动力系统上验证了LEON演化特定的归纳偏置以及算子传播与强迫的互补作用；在两种将潜在预测集成到策略的WAM框架下，LEON均提升了闭环性能与鲁棒性，且在完全替换转移模块时依然有效。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在潜在WAM中，转移实现是一个具有重要影响的架构选择，LEON所体现的演化特定归纳偏置优于以token交互为中心的Transformer式转移实现。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [284] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MM-Spectrum，一个面向多模态多光谱"谱图→分子结构"解析任务的稀疏专家混合（MoE）框架。它通过显式的模态感知路由机制暴露光谱身份信息，并设计共享专家与交互专家、配合异构专家容量，在缓解模态不平衡的同时提取模态特有与跨模态协同信息，抑制噪声干扰。在完整模态、双模态和缺失模态设置下均取得一致且显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多模态光谱测量推断分子结构需要整合互补但高度异质的信号；然而，直接拼接多光谱序列的常见范式会因模态间的显著异质性和模态不平衡而出现反常的性能退化。因此需要一种能更好匹配多光谱不平衡下信息特性的方法。

Method: 提出稀疏 MoE 框架 MM-Spectrum，其核心包括：(1) 显式模态感知路由机制，除 token 内容表示外还将光谱身份信息暴露给路由器；(2) 共享专家与交互专家相结合，配合异构专家容量，以提取多光谱模态特有信息与跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声诱导的干扰。

Result: 在分子结构解析任务的全模态、双模态及缺失模态设置下，MM-Spectrum 均取得一致且显著的性能提升；消融实验与可解释性分析进一步验证了各组件有效性与路由/专家机制的合理性。

Conclusion: 针对多光谱模态不平衡导致的拼接式方法性能退化问题，通过模态感知路由与异构专家设计，MM-Spectrum 能更鲁棒地整合多光谱信号，在多种模态可用性场景下稳定提升分子结构解析性能，为多模态科学数据融合提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [285] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantumBoostNet，一种融合经典骨干网络与量子头的混合经典-量子架构，用于心脏超声视图识别。模型包含一个经典头和一个基于参数化10量子比特电路的量子头，采用两阶段训练及由混合参数控制的适应式头切换。实验表明其在心脏超声视图识别、标准图像分类基准及噪声鲁棒性上均优于SOTA经典与混合经典-量子模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声（超声心动图）中准确识别正确视图或角度是心脏病学成像的关键环节，直接影响解剖解释的准确性、测量可靠性以及临床错误的减少。尽管计算机视觉已有显著进展，但多数先进模型在标准基准上表现良好，而在专业医学影像任务中常因数据噪声水平高而效果欠佳，因此需要能应对此类挑战的新架构。

Method: 提出QuantumBoostNet混合经典-量子架构：以经典骨干网络为基础，配备经典与量子两个输出头，量子头由参数化10量子比特电路实现。训练分两阶段进行，通过监控损失动态的混合参数实现两头的自适应过渡切换。

Result: 大量实验表明，尽管可模拟的量子比特数量有限，QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别任务中持续优于最先进的经典模型及混合经典-量子模型，取得相对最优性能提升；同时在已建立的图像分类基准上表现更优，并展现出对噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持继续发展面向专业医学影像应用的混合经典-量子模型，证明量子增强在噪声较大的医学成像任务中具有实际潜力。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [286] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略（ES）作为 LLM 推理后训练范式的优化行为，从理论、实验和机制层面将其与主流范式 GRPO 对比。作者发现 ES 能保持群体推理覆盖的多样性（verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity），从而获得更高的 Pass@K，而 GRPO 则面临熵坍缩；并提出 GRPO-ES 顺序训练策略兼顾 Pass@1 与 Pass@K。此外，研究发现 ES 的任务性能提升仅由少数大幅更新的稀疏子集贡献，大参数移动并不必然导致功能剧变或灾难性遗忘；最后表明模型越大，ES 所需种群规模越小，从而将 ES 定位为一种独特的推理后训练范式而非 GRPO 的低效替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES 最近成为 LLM 推理中省内存的后训练范式，但其优化行为尚未被充分研究，导致难以界定其相较于主流范式（如 GRPO）的优势范围，因此需要系统考察 ES 的动态与作用机制。

Method: 采用理论分析与实验相结合的方式：理论上证明 verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity 有助于提升 Pass@K；实验上对比 ES 与 GRPO 的熵动态及 Pass@1/Pass@K 表现，并开发顺序式 GRPO-ES 训练策略；通过分析参数漂移与更新幅度子集研究功能稀疏性；通过不同模型规模的种群规模实验研究超参数设计。

Result: 理论、实验均表明 ES 优于 GRPO：ES 带来更广的推理覆盖、更高 Pass@K，且不像 GRPO 那样熵坍缩，同时还能提升 Pass@1；GRPO-ES 顺序策略融合两者优势。任务性能增益仅来自少数大幅更新的稀疏子集，参数大幅移动不等于广泛功能改变，也不必然导致灾难性遗忘；且更大的 LLM 需要更小的种群规模。

Conclusion: ES 应被视为一种独特的推理后训练范式，而非 GRPO 的低效、省内存替代方案；其多样性保持与功能稀疏性特征为 LLM 推理后训练提供了新的设计维度。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [EduRiskX: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework with F-Logic Reasoning for Early Academic Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26107)
*Yu Fu,Yongqi Kang,Yong Zhao,Rongfang Bie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EduRiskX，一个融合时序 Transformer 预测器与 F-Logic 符号推理的神经符号框架，用于在线教育中学生学业风险预测。在 OULAD 数据集上，其准确率达 0.900、F1 达 0.894，平均早期检测周为 9.32，检测率达 94.30%，在预测性能上优于 PatchTST、iTransformer、LSTM、CNN 等基线，同时通过 F-Logic 规则库提供了可解释的结构化诊断依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线教育中预测学生学业风险对及时干预、提升留存率和学习效果至关重要。然而现有模型普遍缺乏早期检测能力且可解释性不足，产生"黑盒"信任危机，阻碍了其在真实教学场景中的落地应用。因此需要一种既能早期识别风险又具备可解释性的预测框架。

Method: 提出神经符号框架 EduRiskX：神经组件采用基于时间注意力的 Transformer 建模纵向学生活动序列，并结合类别加权损失与动态周截断；符号组件依据教育理论（投入理论与学生整合模型）从训练数据中构建 F-Logic 规则库，模拟人类教育者的诊断逻辑，充当数据驱动的专家系统；最后通过基于逻辑回归的融合机制学习神经风险概率与符号置信度各自贡献并加以结合。实验采用严格的学生级 80/10/10 划分。

Result: 在开放大学学习分析数据集（OULAD）上，EduRiskX 在学期末（第 38 周）达到 0.900 的准确率和 0.894 的 F1 值，平均早期检测周为 9.32，检测率为 94.30%。与 SOTA 时序模型（PatchTST、iTransformer）及常见深度学习基线（LSTM、CNN）相比，在相同条件下获得了更高的召回率和更早的风险识别。此外 F-Logic 模块提供了将预测与可观察行为模式及教育理论联系起来的结构化规则解释。

Conclusion: EduRiskX 证明神经-符号融合方法能够在保证学业风险早期检测性能的同时提供可解释的预测依据，缓解"黑盒"信任问题，从而提升了在线教育中风险预测模型的实际可用性与可采纳性。未来可在此框架基础上进一步优化规则挖掘与融合策略，以适配更多教学场景。

Abstract: Predicting students' academic risk in online education is crucial for enabling timely interventions that can improve retention and learning outcomes. However, existing models often suffer from limited early detection capability and insufficient interpretability, leading to a "black-box" trust crisis that hinders their adoption in real-world pedagogical settings. To address these challenges, we propose EduRiskX, a neuro-symbolic framework that integrates a temporal Transformer-based predictor with F-Logic symbolic reasoning. The neural component models longitudinal student activity sequences using temporal attention, class-weighted loss, and dynamic weekly truncation. Acting as a data-driven expert system, an F-Logic rule base -- grounded in established educational theories (Engagement Theory and Student Integration Model) to mimic the diagnostic logic of human educators -- is constructed exclusively from the training data. The neural risk probability and the symbolic confidence score are then combined through a logistic regression-based fusion mechanism that learns the relative contribution of each signal. Experiments on the Open University Learning Analytics Dataset (OULAD) using a strict 80/10/10 student-level split show that EduRiskX achieves an accuracy of 0.900 and an F1-score of 0.894 at the end of the semester (Week 38), with an average early detection week of 9.32 and a detection rate of 94.30 percent. Compared with state-of-the-art time-series models (PatchTST, iTransformer) and common deep learning baselines (LSTM, CNN), EduRiskX yields improved recall and earlier risk identification under identical conditions. Beyond predictive performance, the F-Logic module provides structured rule-based explanations linking predictions to observable behavioral patterns and educational theories.

</details>


### [288] [Standalone LLM and a Pre-specified Agentic Pipeline for Explaining ICU Mortality Predictions: a Feasibility Study on the eICU Demo Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26109)
*Di Zhu,Chen Xie,Haoyun Zhang,Zihan Wei,Ziwei Wang,Jiazhao Shi,Ziyu Wang,Qiyang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了在ICU死亡率预测中，独立大语言模型（LLM）与四步智能体（agentic）流水线生成临床解释的差异。XGBoost模型AUROC为0.855、AUPRC为0.332；在38例分层解释子集上，独立LLM出现1例显式结局泄漏，智能体流水线为零；在14例与SHAP审查重叠的病例中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐和方向一致性更高，而智能体流水线的指南依据、数值特异性及合理性更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型虽能准确预测ICU死亡率，但单纯的特征归因方法难以提供临床所需的叙事性解释，无法直接用于床旁。大语言模型有望弥合这一差距，而多步骤智能体流水线通过分离数据解释、指南核查与最终解释，是合理的扩展方向，但其相对于独立LLM的安全性与临床价值需要验证。

Method: 保留独立LLM与智能体流水线的对照设计，使用本地eICU Demo数据集（2,353例ICU住院，死亡率8.1%），以XGBoost作为预测模型；在37例（原文为38例）分层解释子集上比较两类解释生成方式，并在与SHAP审查重叠的14例上评估对齐度（Jaccard）、方向一致性、指南依据、数值特异性与合理性等指标。

Result: XGBoost取得AUROC 0.855（95% CI 0.796–0.906）与AUPRC 0.332（95% CI 0.217–0.494）。独立LLM出现1例显式结局泄漏，智能体流水线为零。在14例重叠样本中，独立LLM的SHAP对齐（Jaccard 0.171 vs 0.077）和方向一致性（92.9% vs 78.6%）更高；智能体流水线的指南依据（0.762 vs 0.143）、数值特异性（0.236 vs 0.143）与合理性（0.700 vs 0.671）更高。

Conclusion: 智能体式分解可能提升安全性相关的指南依据与患者特定细节，但较弱的归因对齐表明在用于高风险风险解释前，应配合基于归因（如SHAP）的校验机制。

Abstract: Machine-learning models can predict ICU mortality accurately, but feature-attribution methods alone rarely provide the clinical narrative needed for bedside use. Large language models (LLMs) may bridge this gap, and multi-step agentic pipelines are a plausible extension because they separate data interpretation, guideline checking, and final explanation. This revised feasibility study preserves the original standalone-versus-agentic comparison while making the main clinical findings more explicit. Using the retained local eICU Demo artifact set (2,353 ICU stays; 8.1\% mortality), XGBoost achieved an AUROC of 0.855 (95\% CI 0.796--0.906) and an AUPRC of 0.332 (95\% CI 0.217--0.494). On a stratified 38-case explanation subset, the standalone LLM produced 1 explanation with explicit outcome leakage, whereas the four-step agentic pipeline produced none. Among the 14 cases that overlapped with the SHAP review subset, the standalone LLM showed higher SHAP alignment (mean Jaccard 0.171 versus 0.077) and higher direction consistency (92.9\% versus 78.6\%), while the agentic pipeline showed higher guideline grounding (0.762 versus 0.143), higher value specificity (0.236 versus 0.143), and slightly higher plausibility (0.700 versus 0.671). Clinically, the results suggest that agentic decomposition may improve safety-relevant grounding and patient-specific detail, but it should be paired with attribution-based checks before use in high-stakes risk explanation.

</details>


### [289] [Large Models for Battery Prognostics and Health Management: A Review and Future Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26111)
*Jiale Liu,Huan Wang,Weicheng Wang,Rong Zhu,Qiqi Wang,Min Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是首篇系统综述大语言模型（LM）在电池健康管理（BPHM）中应用的综述论文，梳理了Transformer、自监督预训练、多模态数据与PEFT等基础技术，并从缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、融合领域知识提升可解释性、实现系统级自动化四个维度归纳研究进展，进而提出构建协作数据生态、工业智能验证、物理信息可信设计与高效端侧部署的研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BPHM方法（基于物理模型和任务导向的深度学习）面临计算效率与参数化困难、跨域泛化能力不足、依赖大量带标签的run-to-failure数据以及模型可解释性差等长期瓶颈。基于Transformer架构和自监督预训练的大模型为突破这些瓶颈提供了变革性的新范式，因此有必要对这一新兴领域进行系统梳理。

Method: 作为综述性研究，本文首先阐释支撑LM的底层基础技术（Transformer架构、自监督学习、大规模多模态数据集、PEFT参数高效微调），随后从四个关键维度系统归类研究进展：缓解数据稀缺、增强泛化与鲁棒性、整合领域知识以实现可解释性、以及实现系统级自动化；最后针对现存挑战提出面向未来研究的路线图。

Result: 系统总结了LM在BPHM中的现有应用与分类框架，并明确指出当前仍存在的重大挑战，包括数据可获取性、智能验证、可信赖性和部署可行性等方面。

Conclusion: 本文建立了理解和推进LM驱动BPHM的系统性方法论，为研究人员和从业者提供了开发下一代电池管理系统的关键洞见，以支撑电池全生命周期内安全、可靠、自主的运行；同时提出了建设协作数据生态、面向工业应用验证智能、以物理信息设计提升可信度、以及实现高效端侧部署四方面的发展方向。

Abstract: Battery Prognostics and Health Management (BPHM) is critical for ensuring the safe, reliable, and cost-effective operation of batteries across electric vehicles, grid storage, and consumer electronics. Conventional BPHM approaches, including physics-based models and task-centric deep learning methods, face challenges in computational efficiency and parameterization, cross-domain generalization, dependence on extensive labeled run-to-failure data, and model interpretability. Recent Large Models (LMs), built upon Transformer architectures and self-supervised pre-training, offer a transformative new paradigm to overcome these long-standing bottlenecks. This review provides the first comprehensive survey of LM applications in BPHM, systematically examining how these models address challenges in the field. We begin by elucidating the foundational technologies enabling LMs, including Transformer architectures, self-supervised learning, large-scale multimodal datasets, and PEFT techniques. We then categorize recent progress along four critical dimensions: mitigating data scarcity, enhancing generalization and robustness, integrating domain knowledge for interpretability, and enabling system-level automation. Despite promising results, significant challenges remain across data accessibility, intelligence validation, trustworthiness, and deployment feasibility. To guide future research, we propose a roadmap focused on building collaborative data ecosystems, validating intelligence for industrial applications, enhancing trustworthiness with physics-informed designs, and enabling efficient on-device deployment. This review establishes a systematic approach to understand and advance LM-driven BPHM, providing researchers and practitioners with essential insights for developing next-generation battery management systems capable of safe, reliable, and autonomous operation throughout battery lifecycles.

</details>


### [290] [PICasso: An AI-Enabled Design Framework for Autonomous Optimization of Silicon Photonic Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26113)
*Deepak Vungarala,Deniz Najafi,Abdulrahman Aljoudi,Zahra Ghanaatian,Navid Khoshavi,Gourav Datta,Arman Roohi,Mahdi Nikdast,Shaahin Angizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PICasso，一个从自然语言规格说明实现光子集成电路（PIC）自动化综合、验证与优化的 AI 辅助框架。它结合了 NL→YAML→GDS 的结构化生成流水线、PDK 知识注入、自动布局布线、DRC/LVS 校验以及基于 SAX 的光子仿真；并引入包含 36 个参数化设计任务的基准 PIC-Set，在统一评估协议下（含结构/功能 Spec@k、优化效率与扰动鲁棒性等新指标）评测多种主流 LLM。实验表明，相比原生 LLM 生成，PICasso 显著提升端到端规格满足度：高复杂度电路下结构 Spec@3 最高达 92.7%、功能 Spec@3 达 52%，并通过仿真引导优化将平均插损从 4.98 dB 降至 3.25 dB（改善 1.74 dB）。结果证明结构化领域约束、物理验证与仿真反馈能将 LLM 从脆弱的网表生成器转变为可产出可制造版图且运行时间可与人工 GUI 流程竞争的实用 PIC 设计智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子集成电路（PIC）设计高度依赖领域专业知识，现有 LLM 直接生成网表/版图的方案缺乏结构化领域约束与物理验证，生成结果脆弱、难以满足制造要求；同时缺乏统一的、可系统评估 AI 驱动光子设计的基准与评估指标。因此需要一种融合领域知识注入、物理验证与仿真反馈的框架，并建立对应基准以公平评测 LLM 的端到端设计能力。

Method: PICasso 采用 NL→YAML→GDS 的结构化生成流水线，耦合 PDK 感知的知识注入、自动化布局布线、DRC/LVS 物理验证以及基于 SAX 的光子仿真；配套引入包含 36 个参数化任务（覆盖核心光子原语与多组件电路）的基准 PIC-Set，并设计统一评测协议，提出结构/功能 Spec@k、优化效率与扰动鲁棒性等新指标，对多种 SOTA LLM 进行系统的端到端评测与仿真引导优化。

Result: 在 PIC-Set 基准上，PICasso 相比原生 LLM 生成显著提升端到端规格满足度：高复杂度电路的结构 Spec@3 最高达 92.7%，功能 Spec@3 最高达 52%；仿真引导优化将电路平均插损从 4.98 dB 降至 3.25 dB，实现 1.74 dB 的改善；同时运行时间可与人工 GUI 工作流竞争。

Conclusion: 结构化领域约束（PDK 知识注入）、物理验证（DRC/LVS）与仿真反馈共同作用，能将 LLM 从易产生不可靠网表的生成器转变为可产出可制造布局的实用 PIC 设计智能体，在质量、可验证性与运行效率上均优于原生 LLM 生成，并达到接近人工流程的实际可用水平。

Abstract: We present PICasso, an AI-assisted framework for automated synthesis, verification, and optimization of photonic integrated circuits (PICs) from natural-language specifications. PICasso couples a structured NL -> YAML -> GDS generation pipeline with PDK aware knowledge injection, automated placement and routing, DRC/LVS validation, and SAX-based photonic simulation. To systematically evaluate AI-driven photonic design, we introduce PIC-Set, a benchmark of 36 parameterized PIC design tasks spanning core photonic primitives and multi-component circuits. Using PIC-Set, we benchmark several state-of-the-art Large Language Models (LLMs) under a unified evaluation protocol, including new metrics such as structural and functional $Spec@k$, optimization efficiency, and robustness under perturbations. Across the benchmark, PICasso significantly improves end-to-end specification satisfaction compared to vanilla LLM generation. Structural $Spec@3$ reaches up to 92.7% and functional $Spec@3$ up to 52% on high-complexity circuits. In addition, PICasso consistently reduces circuit insertion loss, lowering the mean loss from 4.98 dB to 3.25 dB (1.74 dB improvement) through simulation-guided optimization. These results demonstrate that structured domain constraints, physical verification, and simulation feedback transform LLMs from brittle netlist generators into practical PIC design agents capable of producing manufacturable layouts with competitive runtimes relative to manual GUI-based workflows.

</details>


### [291] [CIFQA: A Deterministic Tool-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Financial Query Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26114)
*Kunjesh Parekh,Anil Kumar Tiwari,Divya Saxena*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CIFQA（计算密集型金融问答）框架，一个确定性的、基于工具的多智能体LLM框架，用于解决金融问答中的多步计算问题。CIFQA将语言理解与数值执行分离，分配专门的智能体负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，而确定性的Python工具负责金融计算和规则应用。在定期存款查询基准上，CIFQA达到95.54%的计算密集型查询准确率和90.87%的整体准确率，显著优于直接LLM基线，并且使用17B开源模型即可超越更大的前沿模型，证明架构设计比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型（LLM）在自然语言任务上表现强劲，但在解决多步金融计算时常常产生数值错误但看似合理的答案。计算密集型金融问答需要精确推理结构化利率、时间条件、数值公式和基于规则的约束，而现有LLM方法难以保证数值可靠性，这正是本工作的出发点。

Method: CIFQA是一个确定性的、基于工具的多智能体LLM框架，其核心设计是将语言理解与数值执行分离：分配专门智能体分别负责查询解释、路由、参数提取、计算规划和响应生成，同时使用确定性的Python工具执行金融计算和规则应用。作者将其实例化用于定期存款查询问答，并在包含定期存款查询的基准上进行了评估。

Result: CIFQA在计算密集型查询上达到95.54%的准确率，整体准确率90.87%，大幅优于直接LLM基线（即使基线获得了完整公式、利率表和基准说明）。消融研究表明，精确利率查找、存期计算、滚动年调整和提前支取逻辑等确定性组件是性能的关键贡献因素。值得注意的是，运行在CIFQA框架内的17B开源模型在相同金融信息下优于规模大得多的前沿模型。

Conclusion: 本文证明，对于计算密集型金融推理任务，架构设计比模型规模更能决定数值可靠性。CIFQA虽然在定期存款查询上评估，但提供了一个可推广到各类计算密集型金融推理任务的通用框架。

Abstract: Calculation-intensive financial question answering requires exact reasoning over structured rates, temporal conditions, numerical formulas, and rule-based constraints. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly on natural language tasks, they often produce numerically incorrect yet plausible answers when solving multi-step financial calculations. To address this limitation, we introduce CIFQA (Calculation-Intensive Financial Query Answering), a deterministic tool-grounded multi-agent LLM framework for financial question answering. CIFQA separates language understanding from numerical execution by assigning specialized agents to query interpretation, routing, parameter extraction, computation planning, and response generation, while deterministic Python-based tools perform financial calculations and rule application. We instantiate CIFQA for fixed deposit query answering and evaluate it on a curated benchmark of fixed deposit queries. CIFQA achieves 95.54% accuracy on calculation-intensive queries and 90.87% overall accuracy, substantially outperforming direct LLM baselines even when provided with complete formulas, rate cards, and benchmark instructions. Ablation studies show that deterministic components such as exact rate lookup, tenure computation, rolling-year adjustment, and premature-withdrawal logic are critical contributors to performance. Notably, a 17B open-source backbone operating within CIFQA outperforms substantially larger frontier models evaluated with the same financial information, demonstrating that architectural design is a more important determinant of numerical reliability than model scale. While evaluated on fixed deposit queries, CIFQA provides a generalizable framework for calculation-intensive financial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [292] [The Artificial Experimentalist: Discovery and Control of Self-Organizing Phenomena with Autotelic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26116)
*Marko Cvjetko,Benedikt Hartl,Michael Levin,Clément Moulin-Frier,Pierre-Yves Oudeyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自生成（autotelic）强化学习的闭环框架 CARL，通过在 Lenia 连续元胞自动机中施加少量局部扰动，自主发现并操控孤子（soliton）等自组织涌现模式，并支持人类以高层指令实时引导，且能零样本泛化到分布外条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有探索元胞自动机等复杂系统的方法大多是开环的：设定初始条件、跑完整仿真、观察结果，执行过程中无法干预。这种范式难以主动发现和定向控制涌现现象，因此需要一种能在仿真过程中进行干预的闭环框架。

Method: 提出闭环的自动智能体实验框架 CARL，基于自生成强化学习：智能体自主采样多样化目标，学习一个目标条件化策略，通过最小、局部的扰动干预复杂系统。该方法在 Lenia（连续元胞自动机）上实例化，并在不同目标、更新规则和随机初始状态下训练。

Result: 实验表明 CARL 具备三项能力：(1) 在多种 Lenia 更新规则下以高于启发式基线（baseline）的比率发现稳定孤子；(2) 用极少量干预学会操控已有孤子的运动方向，说明不仅能创造也能控制自组织模式；(3) 人类可实时通过高层方向命令引导训练好的智能体让孤子穿越迷宫。此外，跨目标、规则和初始状态训练的智能体可对多种分布外条件进行零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了打造"人工实验者"智能体的可行路径：无论是在自主模式下还是人类引导下，这类智能体都能在复杂系统中发现并控制涌现现象，为复杂系统的主动探索与操控提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Existing methods for exploring cellular automata and other complex systems mostly operate in open loop: they set initial conditions, execute a full simulation, and observe the outcome, without intervening during execution. We introduce a closed-loop framework based on autotelic reinforcement learning, in which an agent autonomously samples diverse goals and learns a goal-conditioned policy to intervene in a complex system through minimal, local perturbations. We instantiate this framework on Lenia, a continuous cellular automaton known for life-like self-organizing patterns, in an agentic system we call CARL, and demonstrate three capabilities. First, CARL discovers stable solitons across a wide range of Lenia update rules at a higher rate than heuristic baselines. Second, it learns to steer the movement direction of existing solitons with few interventions, showing that CARL can control self-organizing patterns, not only create them. Third, humans can use trained agents to guide solitons through maze environments in real time by specifying high-level directional commands that the agent translates into low-level interventions. Trained across diverse goals, update rules, and random initial states, the agents acquire policies that generalize zero-shot to various out-of-distribution conditions. These results suggest a path toward artificial experimentalist agents that, autonomously or with human guidance, discover and control emergent phenomena in complex systems.

</details>


### [293] [AI agents in Algorithmic Electricity Markets: On the Emergence of Tacit Collusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26896)
*Jakub Seredyński,Georgios Tsaousoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力市场中自主学习型算法代理的投标策略是否会导致隐性合谋。作者将策略性投标建模为不完全公共监测下的重复博弈，并用多智能体强化学习模拟参与者涌现行为，提出一套超越利润-纳什均衡对比的多维评估标准。实验表明，即使代理从未被指示合谋，它们也可能学会维持高于竞争水平的超竞争结果，支持隐性合谋的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力市场中越来越多的参与者采用基于学习的智能代理制定投标策略，市场正变得算法化。其他领域算法市场的证据表明，单纯通过独立学习就能产生隐性合谋；而电力市场本身具有寡头垄断和小规模参与者反复互动的结构特征，使其天然易受非竞争行为影响。因此作者希望验证一个假设：在参与者行为由自主学习算法控制的电力市场中，隐性合谋可能自发出现。

Method: 将策略性投标建模为带不完美公共监测的重复博弈；采用多智能体强化学习来模拟参与者的涌现行为；提出一套多维评判标准（超越单纯与纳什均衡的利润对比）来判定行为是否构成隐性合谋。

Result: 实验结果表明该风险在电力市场中是现实存在的：在某些场景下，即使代理从未被指示合谋，它们确实学会了维持支持隐性合谋指标的超竞争结果。

Conclusion: 电力市场中由自主学习算法控制的参与者可能自发涌现隐性合谋，这对电力市场的竞争性监管构成警示；需要超越利润对比的多维判据来识别此类行为，并可能要求监管层面加以干预。

Abstract: As electricity market participants increasingly adopt learning-based agents for their bidding strategies, electricity markets are becoming algorithmic. Evidence from algorithmic markets in other domains shows that tacit collusion can arise purely through independent learning. Moreover, electricity markets are typically oligopolistic and feature repeated interaction among a small number of participants, making them structurally susceptible to non-competitive behavior. In the face of these observations, this paper investigates the hypothesis that tacit collusion may emerge in electricity markets where participants' actions are controlled by autonomous learning-based algorithms. We model strategic bidding as a repeated game with imperfect public monitoring, and model the participants' emergent behavior using multi-agent reinforcement learning. We propose a multi-dimensional set of criteria (going beyond profit comparisons against Nash equilibria) to assess whether the resulting behavior constitutes tacit collusion. Our experimental results showcase that such a danger is realistic for electricity markets: there are cases where agents do learn to sustain supra-competitive outcomes that are supportive of tacit collusion indicators, even though the agents were never instructed to collude.

</details>


### [294] [The Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox Quantifying Net Energy Loss in on-Device Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26134)
*Jaeik Jeong,Tai-Yeon Ku,Wan-Ki Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了能源预测中的"精度-效率悖论"：高精度的能源预测模型虽能提高预测精度，但因其推理能耗和电池老化带来的能量损耗，反而可能导致净能量赤字。作者提出一个总拥有成本（TCO）框架，将推理能耗与电池老化统一视为能量损失，以最小化净能量损失为目标，并证明在热敏感的边缘环境下，复杂架构带来的精度收益往往被其高运行强度造成的总能量损失所抵消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源预测的目标是最大化精度以减少能源浪费，这一目标同样适用于军事系统等关键任务边缘环境。然而，作者发现高精度能源预测模型可能因边缘AI的推理能耗和电池老化而引发净能量赤字，即存在"精度-效率悖论"——精度提升反而导致总体能量效率下降，因此需要重新审视该问题。

Method: 提出一个面向能源预测的总拥有成本（TCO）框架，用于最小化净能量损失。该框架将推理能耗与电池老化统一纳入能量损失的范畴，因为电池退化代表了系统未来储能能力的物理耗散，从而把系统全生命周期的能量代价整合为统一的优化目标。

Result: 实验证明，在热敏感的边缘环境中，复杂架构凭借更高精度所节省的能量，往往被其高运行强度所导致的总能量损失（推理能耗加电池老化）所超越，从而验证了精度-效率悖论的存在，并说明单纯追求预测精度并不利于净能量效率。

Conclusion: 能源预测模型的评估不应只以预测精度为准，而应从TCO视角综合考虑推理能耗与电池老化带来的能量损失。在热敏感边缘场景下，应优先选择运行强度与总体能量效率更优的架构，以避免"高精度反而导致净能量赤字"的悖论，从而真正实现能量效率目标。

Abstract: Energy forecasting aims to maximize accuracy to ensure energy efficiency by reducing energy waste, an objective that applies equally to on-device forecasting for mission-critical edge environments, including military systems. However, this paper identifies the Accuracy-Efficiency Paradox: high-precision energy forecasting models can ironically trigger a net energy deficit. This stems from both edge AI's inference energy consumption and battery aging. We propose a Total Cost of Ownership (TCO) framework for energy forecasting, designed to minimize net energy loss.
