Conclusion: 这些证据确立了被测软件契约上的实现一致性，而不宣称报告质量、预测准确性或投资收益方面的优越性。

Abstract: Large language models can summarize financial information, but an operational stock-research system must first assemble heterogeneous evidence, expose unavailable data and model capabilities, and control how generated opinions affect a final report. We present DSA, an evidence-aware orchestration framework for multi-market stock research with large language model (LLM) agents. DSA organizes the workflow into evidence acquisition, structured context construction, model-routed analysis, optional role and Strategy Skill reasoning, and report generation with selected context and diagnostics. A default report profile and an optional agentic profile share evidence and model-routing services but use profile-specific output validation and risk safeguards. In the agentic profile, core role outputs are processed by role-specific parsers, whereas Strategy Skill opinions undergo an additional signal-eligibility partition before synthesis; disagreement is supplied explicitly to the decision agent, followed by a conservative risk override. The reference implementation includes six regional market paths, fifteen bundled Strategy Skills, hosted and local model routes, and multiple execution and delivery surfaces. At a frozen software snapshot, a selected manifest of 1,457 portable offline backend contract tests passed; 596 cases were retrospectively mapped to six contract families central to the reported LLM-agent architecture. This evidence establishes implementation conformance for the tested software contracts, not superior report quality, forecasting accuracy, or investment returns.

</details>


### [359] [ASIL: Replacing Screenshot-and-Click with Structured State and Semantic Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26991)
*Rui Xie,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASIL（Agent-软件交互层），一种以结构化JSON观测和可执行代码的语义动作取代截图-点击的智能体原生软件操作接口，在15个应用、300个单应用和80个多应用任务上达到80%以上的成功率，并在训练场景下通过SFT和RL显著提升小模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强大的代码智能体可执行脚本、调用工具和管理文件，但许多重要应用仍主要通过图形用户界面访问。作者论证截图-点击对软件操作智能体而言是低效接口：截图状态不完整，GUI动作脆弱、语义弱且不适合长程规划，因此需要更高效的智能体-软件交互方式。

Method: 作者引入ASIL（Agent-Software Interaction Layer），一种智能体原生接口，通过结构化JSON观测和代码可执行的语义动作暴露软件功能，并为每个应用实现"最深可行访问路径"（即直接对接底层API/协议，而非模拟GUI）。论文在15个应用中实例化ASIL，并构建包含300个单应用和80个多应用任务的基准。

Result: 在闭源模型上，ASIL成功率超过80%，且每个任务平均动作数少于5个。对比修复运行时和50步截图预算下的截图-点击控制，同一任务严格成功率仅为6.6%和26.6%（在较易的OSWorld可比子集上为15.0%和53.3%）。与匹配任务上的应用原生接口相比，ASIL超过LibreOffice UNO API达28-38个严格成功率点，但仅与draw.io的MCP内容契约持平。结构化模态利于训练：小规模SFT将Qwen3.5-2B从58.0提升至72.1，Qwen3.5-9B从66.6提升至80.4；资源受限的在线RL进一步将其提升至74.4和82.2。

Conclusion: ASIL证明了面向软件操作的智能体原生接口（结构化观测+语义动作+深度访问路径）相比截图-点击范式在成功率、动作效率和可训练性上均有显著优势，为软件操作智能体提供了一种更高效的交互范式，并展示了与开源小模型结合的可扩展性。

Abstract: Powerful code agents can execute scripts, call tools, and manage files, yet many important applications remain accessible primarily through graphical user interfaces. We argue that screenshot-and-click is an inefficient interface for software-operating agents: screenshots are state-incomplete, and GUI actions are brittle, semantically weak, and poorly matched to long-horizon planning. We introduce ASIL (Agent-Software Interaction Layer), an agent-native interface that exposes software through structured JSON observations and code-executable semantic actions, realized through the deepest feasible access path for each application. We instantiate ASIL across 15 applications and a benchmark of 300 single-application and 80 multi-application tasks. ASIL reaches above 80 with closed models while executing fewer than five actions per task. Under a repaired runtime and a 50-step screenshot budget, the same tasks yield 6.6 and 26.6 strict success under screenshot-and-click control, rising to 15.0 and 53.3 on an easier OSWorld-comparable band. Against application-native interfaces on matched tasks, ASIL exceeds LibreOffice's UNO API by 28-38 strict points but only matches draw.io's MCP content contract. The structured modality also suits training: small-scale SFT raises Qwen3.5-2B from 58.0 to 72.1 and Qwen3.5-9B from 66.6 to 80.4, and resource-limited on-policy RL further raises them to 74.4 and 82.2.

</details>


### [360] [A Multi-Modal AI Framework for Real-Time Queue Prediction, Management and Optimisation in Intelligent Border Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27010)
*Varvara Mama,Eleni Veroni,Nikolaos Kapsalis,Christos D. Nikolopoulos,Anargyros T. Baklezos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向边境管控的多模态人工智能框架，融合LSTM队列预测、模型预测控制（MPC）与调度优化，实现动态队列管理、资源优化和实时控制决策，在合成数据上相比ARIMA和基于规则的方法将队列预测误差降低35%、平均等待时间降低30%、吞吐量提升约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有边境管控的运营队列管理系统大多基于静态数据进行操作，难以应对动态交通状况和不确定性，无法实现最优性能，因此需要一个能结合实时交通条件进行预测和资源优化的智能管理方案。

Method: 提出一个多模态人工智能框架：集成异构数据源并通过统一表示呈现，采用LSTM网络进行队列预报；结合MPC与调度优化推导可执行的管控策略，并呈现给边境管控人员；使用模拟真实交通的合成数据进行评估。

Result: 评估结果表明，与ARIMA和基于规则的方法相比，所提方法将队列预测误差降低最高35%，平均等待时间降低30%，平均吞吐量提升近20%。

Conclusion: 实验结果展示了将AI架构与优化技术相结合用于主动、自适应边境交通管理的有效性和高效性，证实了该框架在动态、不确定环境下优于传统方法的潜力。

Abstract: In the present work an efficient border control management procedure is proposed. Compared to operational queue management systems, whose operations are based on mostly static data, the proposed work takes into account dynamic traffic conditions, thus enabling optimal performance, even in cases of uncertainty. To this end, we are proposing a multi-modal Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework, tailored to th needs of border control systems, which enables real-time queue prediction, management, and resource optimization. The novel proposed approach integrates heterogeneous data sources and presents them through a unified representation by employing Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks for queue forecasting. Furthermore, it leverages Model Predictive Control (MPC) and scheduling optimization to derive actionable control policies, which in turn can be presented to border control officers. The proposed work has been evaluated using synthetic data simulating realistic traffic. The evaluation results demonstrate that the proposed method reduces queue prediction error by up to 35% and average waiting time by 30%. Accordingly, the average throughput increases by nearly 20%, compared to ARIMA and rule-based methods. The abovementioned results show the effectiveness and efficiency of combining AI architectures with optimization techniques for proactive and adaptive border traffic management.

</details>


### [361] [Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27044)
*Wei-Yao Wang,Kazuya Tateishi,Shuyang Cui,Christian Simon,Takashi Shibuya,Shusuke Takahashi,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniUE，首个支持文本、视频、音频统一嵌入空间且支持多模态交互查询（文本、视觉区域、音频片段）的通用嵌入器，并引入 OmniCHOIR 基准，在多项基准上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态表示学习正从传统双塔架构转向基于大语言模型的嵌入器，但现有方法主要聚焦语言和图像模态，且用户条件化交互也以这两类模态为主，缺乏面向文本、视频、音频的统一模态表示学习和支持用户多样化交互（如视觉区域、音频片段）的通用嵌入能力。

Method: OmniUE 利用专用可学习 token 的中间层表示，构建跨文本、视频、音频的统一嵌入空间；通过视觉和音频分段器处理多种用户交互输入，并与 Omni-LLM 集成，通过上下文聚合生成用户条件化的任意到任意（any-to-any）嵌入。同时，构建 OmniCHOIR 基准，用于评测基于文本、视频、音频及单模态或多模态交互提示的组合音频检索能力。

Result: OmniUE 在多种模态上均超越最先进基线：文本交互视频基准（MMEB-v2-video）平均提升 10.5%，音频任务（MAEB）提升 1.1%，视觉交互基准（SCaR）提升 83.7%，以及自建 OmniCHOIR 基准提升 24.1%。

Conclusion: 联合推进全模态表示学习与全能交互查询是实现通用嵌入器的关键方向，本文工作为多模态通用嵌入器的发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Multimodal representation learning has been shifting from traditional two-tower architectures to large language model (LLM)-based embedders due to their strong instruction-following capabilities. Despite this progress, existing approaches primarily focus on language and image modalities, which also remain the dominant modalities for user-conditioned interactions in current embedders. In this paper, we propose the first Omni-Interactive Universal Embedder (OmniUE), which not only learns a unified embedding space across text, video, and audio by leveraging intermediate-layer representations from dedicated learnable tokens, but also supports omni-interactive querying, enabling users to provide inputs in the form of text, visual regions of interest, and audio spans. Within OmniUE, visual and audio segmenters process diverse user interactions and integrate them with an omni-LLM to produce user-conditioned any-to-any embeddings via context aggregation. To evaluate OmniUE's omni-interactive capabilities, we introduce OmniCHOIR, benchmarking models for omni-interactive compositional audio retrieval based on the given text, video, and audio as well as unimodal or multimodal interaction prompts. OmniUE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art baselines across diverse modalities, with average improvements of 10.5% on textual-interactive video benchmarks (MMEB-v2-video), 1.1% on audio tasks (MAEB), 83.7% on visual-interactive benchmarks (SCaR), and 24.1% on our omni-interactive OmniCHOIR benchmark. We believe that jointly advancing omni-modal representation learning and omni-interactive querying paves the way toward universal embedders.

</details>


### [362] [A Contract-Centered Architecture for Scalable and Manageable Agentic Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27086)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Zhenghe Hou,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向企业级AI部署的契约化运行时架构：将跨团队协调问题抽象为四类责任对象（Skill能力资产、Harness运行时编译与治理器、Scaffold执行/控制边界与NFR所有者、以及CIO治理的数据基底），并以"成本感知的能力-容量可分离性"假说P1为核心，配以六个可测设计条件和一种簇-周期随机交叉实验协议来判定该假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级AI部署本质上是跨业务单元、AI团队、测试、平台工程、基础设施、安全、运维与数据治理的协调问题。现有use-case基准只能判断单个智能体能否完成单任务，无法回答能力、模型、运行时机制、容量与企业数据应如何被共同拥有、变更、准入和取证，因此缺乏一个能系统化处理这些相互依赖关系的架构与验证框架。

Method: 提出四个共享的组织契约对象：Skill（可复用、可版本化的能力与工作流资产）、Harness（运行时编译器和治理器）、Scaffold（执行/控制边界与NFR所有者）、以及独立于技术栈、由CIO治理语义和遥测的数据基底。运行时核心为A=<S,H,X>，数据基底在栈外。核心贡献是可证伪假说P1（成本感知的能力-容量可分离性），并定义了六个设计条件作为量化的义务（覆盖面、违规、不确定性、成本与排除项），从而决定P1是否可判定。验证方案采用簇-周期随机交叉实验（均衡顺序、重置/洗脱、重复种子与故障场景、簇感知的不确定性），输出四种状态：支持、证伪、条件性工程、不确定。

Result: 论文的产出包括：契约约束的运行时架构、源保留的数据基底、以及可证伪的测量协议/四状态判定机制。论文明确声明尚无完成的实现、实验、数据集或实测结果，属于架构与协议层面的理论性贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作主张面向企业AI部署的联合变更问题需要以"可证伪假说+组织契约+受控实验"的方式来治理，而非仅靠单任务基准。核心意义在于把能力变更与容量变更的交互封装成可预先登记等价域（P1），通过六个可测条件把抽象的跨团队协调转化为可判定的测量义务；其局限是尚无实证结果，所有主张仍待实验验证。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployment is a coordination problem across business units, application and AI teams, testing, platform engineering, infrastructure, security, operations, and data governance. Use-case benchmarks show whether one agent completes one task, but not how changing capabilities, models, runtime mechanisms, capacity, and enterprise data should be owned, changed, admitted, or evidenced together.
  We present four responsibility objects as shared organizational contracts: Skill (reusable, versioned capability and workflow asset), Harness (runtime compiler and governor), Scaffold (execution/control boundary and NFR owner), and a stack-external data substrate under independent CIO-governed semantics and telemetry. The runtime core is A = <S, H, X>, with the data substrate outside that stack.
  The central contribution is one bounded, falsifiable hypothesis, P1 (cost-aware capability-capacity separability): within a declared operating region, changing activated capability preserves the capacity-response interaction within a preregistered equivalence margin, while changing compatible Scaffold capacity preserves capability semantics up to a non-inferiority margin, and the required controls stay within a declared enforcement budget. Six design conditions become measured obligations whose coverage, violations, uncertainty, cost, and exclusions determine whether P1 is decidable.
  We propose a cluster-period randomized crossover experiment (balanced order, reset/washout, repeated seeds and failure regimes, cluster-aware uncertainty) with a four-state verdict: supported, falsified, conditional-engineering, or inconclusive. This paper contributes a contract-bounded runtime architecture, a source-preserving data substrate, and a falsifiable measurement protocol. It reports no completed implementation, experiment, dataset, or measured result.

</details>


### [363] [pro-team at LLMs4OL 2026 Tasks Flagship and Reuse: Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Vocabulary-Constrained Filtering for Ontology Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27101)
*Shivam Mishra,Dhannu Ram Meena,Muneendra Ojha,Krishna Pratap Singh,Kuldeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向LLMs4OL 2026挑战赛的离线检索增强少样本提示流水线，采用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct搭配all-MiniLM-L6-v2检索演示样本，结合左截断上下文窗口策略与确定性词汇约束过滤，在Task B上取得Semantic Graph Similarity 0.8692、Term-Typing F1 0.9200、Taxonomy Discovery F1 0.8540，在Task A上取得0.7416；但未能提取任何非分类关系，暴露了封闭、面向分类学的关系词汇表的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型取得显著进展，本体学习仍具挑战：LLM会幻觉领域术语、产生不一致格式，且偏向层级关系而非关联关系。作者针对LLMs4OL 2026挑战赛的端到端旗舰任务（Task A）与本体扩展复用任务（Task B）开展研究。

Method: 采用离线检索增强少样本提示流水线：使用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct作为生成模型，all-MiniLM-L6-v2用于演示检索（Task A取top-5示例，Task B取top-2）；采用左截断的上下文窗口策略以在长提示中保留任务指令；对Task B生成的三元组执行确定性的词汇约束过滤，仅保留至少一个端点属于样本封闭术语/类型词汇表的三元组，并去除初始本体的重复项。

Result: Task B达到Semantic Graph Similarity 0.8692、Term-Typing F1 0.9200、Taxonomy Discovery F1 0.8540；Task A达到Semantic Graph Similarity 0.7416。但未提取到任何非分类关系。

Conclusion: 实验表明该流水线在术语类型划分与分类发现上表现良好，但无法提取非分类关系，凸显了封闭且面向分类学的关系词汇表带来的根本性局限——此类词汇表约束限制了对关联关系的建模能力。

Abstract: Ontology learning from text remains challenging despite significant progress in Large Language Models (LLMs), which can hallucinate domain terms, produce inconsistent formats, and favor hierarchical over associative relations. In the LLMs4OL 2026 Challenge, we address both the End-to-End Flagship Task (Task A) and Ontology Extension Reuse Task (Task B) using an offline retrieval-augmented few-shot prompting pipeline. Our system employs Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct with all-MiniLM-L6-v2 for demonstration retrieval, selecting the top-5 examples for Task A and top-2 for Task B. A left-truncated context-windowing strategy preserves task instructions within long prompts. For Task B, generated triples undergo deterministic vocabulary-constrained filtering, retaining triples when at least one endpoint belongs to the sample's closed term/type vocabulary and removing duplicates of the initial ontology. The approach achieves Semantic Graph Similarity of 0.8692, Term-Typing F1 of 0.9200, and Taxonomy Discovery F1 of 0.8540 on Task B, while Task A achieves 0.7416 Semantic Graph Similarity. However, no non-taxonomic relations are extracted, highlighting limitations of closed, taxonomy-oriented relation vocabularies.

</details>


### [364] [LAAF: A Layered Accountability Architecture Framework for LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27102)
*Prachi Chaturvedi,Shahnawaz Ahmad,Ehsan Nowroozi,Muhammad Waqas,George Loukas,Alireza Jolfaei,Lucas Cordeiro,Pierre Dantas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM在医院、法院、银行等高风险场景中的问责问题进行了系统综述（PRISMA方法，122项研究+12份监管文件），提炼出五维问责关系与四类机制家族，并构建了四层分类装置（LAAF架构），将其映射到EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001等监管框架，指出了四个持续性缺口与五个结构性张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医疗、司法、金融等公共服务领域被当作权威来源使用，其流畅而自信的输出可能缺乏依据或包含错误。当此类输出导致伤害时，缺乏明确的问责主体与可追溯、可解释、可执行的问责机制，这是亟需系统性梳理的理论与实践空白。

Method: 遵循PRISMA指南对5个数据库进行检索（2022年1月至2026年3月），从4,512条记录中纳入122项原始研究及12份作为一手来源的监管/标准文件，围绕四个评审问题展开；通过四层分类装置（来源、应用逻辑、人类监督、治理与救济，并以可追溯性、角色清晰度、持续监控为横切维度）对语料进行解读，并与多个监管框架进行映射。

Result: 将问责整合为"行为者-场合关系"的社会技术概念并解析为五个维度；综合了四类机制家族（技术控制、人类监督、组织治理、文档化与可追溯性），并给出成熟度评估；识别出四个持续缺口（人类监督规定不足、缺乏统一的问责度量、学科脱节、实证评估有限）和五个现有工具均未解决的结构性张力；最终形成一体化问责架构LAAF，并与OWASP LLM Top 10（2025）对齐。

Conclusion: 该综述在理论和监管层面提供了系统的问责框架与缺口图景，并提出了LAAF作为整合性架构；但作者明确指出LAAF是对现有证据的综合而非经过验证的制品，强调未来需要实证检验、跨学科协作以及统一的问责度量标准。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) operate in hospitals, courtrooms, banks, and public service desks, where fluent, confident outputs are treated as authoritative even when ungrounded or incorrect. When such an output contributes to harm, who is answerable, and through what mechanisms can responsibility be traced, explained, and acted upon? Following PRISMA guidance, five databases were searched from January 2022 to March 2026 against four review questions; of 4,512 records identified, 122 primary studies were included, together with 12 regulatory and standards documents analysed as primary sources. The review consolidates a sociotechnical account of accountability as an actor-forum relation resolved into five dimensions, and synthesises mechanisms across four families: technical controls, human oversight, organisational governance, and documentation and traceability, each with a maturity assessment. The corpus is read through a four-layer classification device spanning provenance, application logic, human oversight, and governance and redress, cross-cut by traceability, role clarity, and continuous monitoring. Both are mapped onto the EU AI Act, whose high-risk obligations have applied since 2 August 2026, the NIST AI RMF with its Generative AI Profile, ISO/IEC 42001, and sectoral guidance in healthcare, consumer finance, education, and the public sector. Four persistent gaps emerge: under-specification of human oversight, absence of shared accountability metrics, disciplinary disconnection, and limited empirical evaluation, alongside five structural tensions that no surveyed instrument resolves. The review closes by consolidating the classification device into an integrated accountability architecture, LAAF, with cybersecurity aligned to the OWASP LLM Top 10 (2025); it is a synthesis of the surveyed evidence rather than a validated artefact.

</details>


### [365] [TransMeme: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Cultural Meme Transcreation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27127)
*Jingyi Zheng,Yule Liu,Zifan Peng,Tianyi Hu,Yuemeng Zhao,Xinhu Zheng,Xinlei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对跨文化网络迷因转创（cross-cultural meme transcreation）任务，提出一个多智能体框架，通过文化适配、目标文本改写、修订与条件性视觉调整等专门智能体的协调，联合解决文化知识理解、意图与语气保持、多模态一致性三大挑战，并在中英双向迷因转创的人评与LLM裁判评估中全面超越基线，指出剩余瓶颈在于幽默重构与图文对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络迷因是多模态在线交流的普遍形式，但用户来自不同语言文化背景，跨文化适配迷因对促进理解至关重要。与普通翻译或纯文本改写不同，迷因转创需同时保持交际意图、针对目标受众适配文化依赖含义并维持图文一致性，现有工作缺乏对此任务的显式分析与系统性方案。

Method: 首先对跨文化迷因转创进行显式任务分析，归纳出三个核心挑战：文化特定知识理解、意图与语气保持、多模态一致性。基于此提出多智能体框架，由文化适配、目标文本改写、修订和条件性视觉调整等专门智能体协同工作，并通过协调式反馈加强目标文本适配，以处理需要更深层文化或视觉干预的困难样本。

Result: 在中英双向迷因转创上进行人评与LLM-as-a-Judge评估，方法一致超越所有基线。人评在四个维度全部最佳，较最强基线平均提升33.1%；LLM裁判中Top-1排名率最高（60%，次优基线为26%）。消融表明各组件均有贡献；错误分析显示剩余瓶颈在于幽默重构与图文对齐，而非简单的文化知识缺口。

Conclusion: 多智能体协同框架能有效处理跨文化迷因转创中的文化、意图与多模态一致性问题，但当前方法在幽默迁移方面仍有不足，未来的工作应聚焦于幽默转移与图文对齐的进一步改进。

Abstract: Internet memes are a pervasive form of multimodal online communication; however, such communication often involves users from diverse linguistic and cultural backgrounds. Therefore, adapting memes across cultures and languages is a central challenge for enabling mutual understanding in online communication. Unlike ordinary translation or standalone text rewriting, cross-cultural meme transcreation must jointly preserve communicative intent, adapt culture-dependent meaning for the target audience, and maintain coherence between text and image. In this work, we first provide an explicit task analysis of cross-cultural meme transcreation and identify three core challenges: culture-specific knowledge understanding, intent and tone preservation, and multimodal consistency. Based on this analysis, we propose a multi-agent framework with specialized agents that are coordinated to address these challenges through cultural adaptation, target text rewriting, revision, and conditional visual adjustment. The framework strengthens target text adaptation with coordinated feedback to handle difficult cases that require deeper cultural or visual intervention. We evaluate the framework on bidirectional Chinese-English meme transcreation using both human evaluation and LLM-as-a-Judge. Our method consistently outperforms all baselines across both evaluation settings. In human evaluation, it achieves the best performance on all four dimensions and delivers a 33.1% average improvement over the strongest baseline, while in LLM-as-a-Judge, it attains the highest Top-1 ranking rate (60% versus 26% for the second-best baseline). Further analysis indicates that each component contributes to the performance. Our error analysis suggests that the remaining bottlenecks lie in humor reconstruction and image-text alignment rather than simple cultural knowledge gaps, pointing to future work on humor transfer.

</details>


### [366] [GRAIN: Bridging Name and Narrative Shifts in Real-World Graph Reasoning through Invariance-Rewarded Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27142)
*Zike Yuan,Han Zhang,Jianzhi Yan,Le Liu,Cai Ke,Huozhi Zhou,Jian Xie,Jiran Yin,Yukun Cao,Yue Yu,Hui Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 GRAIN，一个基于强化学习优化的单智能体框架，通过结构不变性奖励引导语义解析与工具执行流水线，使 LLM 学习稳健的文本到图结构映射；并配套提出基准 GRIT。GRAIN 在准确率上超越多智能体基线 16.45%，延迟降低约 24%，并将 OOD 差距从 15.77% 减半至 7.80%，展现出优异的图结构泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在标准化图任务上表现良好，但对节点标识符和任务表述的真实世界变化十分脆弱：从嘈杂文本中提取拓扑结构极易失败，模型往往过拟合表面语言模式；现有的解析缓解方案依赖多智能体系统，但延迟过高无法实际部署。

Method: 提出单智能体框架 GRAIN，通过强化学习优化；将推理建模为语义解析加工具执行的流水线，并由结构不变性奖励（Structure Invariance Reward）引导——将提取出的中间图与真实拓扑进行校验，迫使 LLM 学习鲁棒的文本到结构映射而非记忆语言伪影。同时引入基准 GRIT 用于评估对语言变化的敏感性。

Result: GRAIN 在准确率上比多智能体基线高出 16.45%，延迟降低约 24%；在结构泛化上表现卓越，将 SFT 模型的分布外（OOD）差距从 15.77% 减半至 7.80%，并在超出训练分布的大规模图上保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GRAIN 证明通过结构不变性奖励的强化学习，单智能体能够同时实现高准确率、低延迟和强结构泛化，有效替代代价高昂的多智能体解析方案，为 LLM 在真实图任务中的可靠性提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Despite their potential in standardized graph tasks, Large Language Models (LLMs) remain brittle to real-world shifts in node identifiers and task formulation. While deterministic graph tools are invariant to such shifts, extracting topological structures from noisy text is highly fragile for LLMs, which often overfit to surface patterns. Moreover, mitigating these parsing failures via multi-agent systems incurs prohibitive latency. To address this, we propose GRAIN, a single-agent framework optimized via reinforcement learning. GRAIN models reasoning as a semantic parsing and tool-execution pipeline, guided by a Structure Invariance Reward. By validating extracted intermediate graphs against ground-truth topologies, this reward forces the LLM to learn robust text-to-structure mappings rather than memorizing linguistic artifacts. We also introduce GRIT, a benchmark evaluating sensitivity to such linguistic shifts. GRAIN outperforms multi-agent baselines by 16.45\% in accuracy with approximately 24\% lower latency. Furthermore, it demonstrates superior structural generalization, halving the out-of-distribution (OOD) gap of SFT models (from 15.77\% to 7.80\%) and maintaining robustness on large-scale graphs beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [367] [Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering for Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27144)
*Xiang Wang,Zhijun Cheng,Zhenyu Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于雅可比多项式图滤波网络的图异常检测方法（JPGFN），通过特征分离变换、自适应雅可比多项式图滤波和节点标签约束三个模块，分别解决现有频域滤波方法无法自适应频域分布、忽略节点特征内部各属性重要性、以及标签利用不充分的问题，在多个真实数据集上显著优于主流方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于频域滤波的图异常检测方法存在三大挑战：一是使用静态基函数构造图滤波器，无法有效适应图数据的频域分布；二是未充分考虑节点特征向量中各属性的重要性信息，导致细粒度信息丢失；三是节点标签利用不充分。这些问题制约了图异常检测的性能提升。

Method: 提出JPGFN方法，包含三个核心模块：(1) 特征分离变换网络（FSTNN），通过对不同维度的节点特征进行特征分离和非线性变换，以更好地学习细粒度节点特征；(2) 基于雅可比多项式构建的自适应图滤波模块，用于自适应捕获图信号中复杂的频域特征；(3) 节点标签约束模块，促进节点标签的利用以增强图异常检测性能。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的JPGFN方法在性能上显著优于主流图异常检测方法。

Conclusion: JPGFN通过自适应滤波、细粒度特征学习和标签约束三方面改进，有效解决了现有频域滤波GAD方法的缺陷，在图异常检测任务上取得了领先性能。

Abstract: In recent years, graph anomaly detection (GAD) based on frequency-domain filtering have achieved promising results. However, existing approaches still face three major challenges: First, they use static basic function to constructed graph filter which cannot effectively adapt to the frequency-domain distribution of graph data. Second, they fail to adequately consider the importance information of each attribute in the node feature vector, leading to the loss of fine-grained information. Third, they insufficiently utilize node labels for GAD. To address these issues, this paper proposes a novel graph anomaly detection method called JPGFN (Feature Transformation Enhanced Jacobi Polynomial Graph Filtering Network). First, a Feature Separation Transformation Network (FSTNN) is developed to better learn fine-grained node features by feature separation and applying nonlinear transformations to node features across different dimensions. Second, an adaptive Jacobi polynomial graph filtering module is constructed based on Jacobi polynomials to adaptively capture complex frequency-domain features of graph signals. Finally, a node label constraint module is developed to facilitate the use of node labels and enhance the performance of GAD. Experimental results on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms mainstream approaches.

</details>


### [368] [Calibrated Enough to Know, Not Calibrated to Act: Fabricated Evidence Makes LLM Agents Commit to the Unknowable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27167)
*Pranav Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现：面对专业外观的市场面板，LLM 智能体在不可预测的问题上做出方向性判断的比率会大幅飙升（12个前沿模型由6.5%升至54.0%），即使面板数据完全伪造也依然显著生效——真正触发自信行动的不是信息，而是包装所赋予的权威性。该缺陷并非源于模型无能、信念改变或判断缺失，而是集中于"行动/不行动"的门控环节，且主要存在于少数模型中。该门控可通过540个合成样本的监督微调在3B模型上被训练至0.0%承诺率并泛化到三个未见领域，但对响应格式极其敏感：一旦格式剥夺了推理空间，模型就会在原本能答对的问题上自信犯错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM智能体在面对看似专业的市场面板信息时，是否会出于表面权威而非真实信息，在本质不可预测的问题上轻率做出方向性判断，并试图定位这一失效的深层原因（能力、信念、判断还是行动门控），从而为可控部署提供依据。

Method: 实验设计采用递进式证据升级（bare question→带面板的完整展示），在12个前沿模型上对比真实数据与完全伪造数据的效果；通过匹配可回答问题、可比对的主观概率陈述、以及"可知道性"分类任务来逐一排除能力、信念与判断层面的解释；最后对3B模型用540个合成案例（骰子、硬币、罐子、计时器等）进行监督微调，并在三个未见领域上检验迁移，同时考察不同响应格式对门控稳健性的影响。

Result: 承诺率随证据升级从6.5%升至54.0%；伪造面板仍将承诺率从24.5%提升至36.8%，与真实数据产生的37.6%在统计上无法区分。在匹配的可答问题上模型几乎全对；陈述概率随梯度几乎不动且差于气候学基线；模型在90%情况下正确判定问题不可知，却仍在其中0.4%的情形下承诺。门控失效集中在少数模型。微调后原始案例承诺率降至0.0%，并迁移至三个未见领域；但在不留推理空间的刚性格式下，门控失效，模型自信且答错。

Conclusion: LLM的过度承诺并非源于信息本身、能力不足或信念扭曲，而是"行动/不行动"门控在e权威包装下的失效。该门控可分离、可训练，却高度依赖上下文格式而脆弱——部署时必须同时认识到其可训练性与上下文敏感性（gate is trainable and context-fragile），否则在受限提示格式中模型会自信地犯错。

Abstract: An LLM agent shown a professional-looking market panel commits to a directional call on a provably unpredictable question far more often than one asked the bare question: across 12 frontier models, commitment rises from 6.5% to 54.0% as evidence is escalated. It commits just as readily when every number on the panel is invented: fabricating the entire display, so nothing the model can see is true except the question itself, still lifts commitment from 24.5% to 36.8%, statistically indistinguishable from the 37.6% produced by genuine market data. What unlocks confident action is not information but the authority of its packaging. The failure is narrow and locatable. Incapacity is not the answer: on matched answerable questions attached to the same panels, the same models answer essentially always, at near-perfect accuracy. Nor is it belief - stated probabilities barely move across the gradient that swings action by 48 points, and score worse than a climatological baseline. Missing judgment isn't it either: asked to classify a question's knowability before acting, models call it irreducible 90% of the time and then commit on just 0.4% of those. The act/don't-act gate is what fails, and the effect is concentrated in a few models rather than universal. Because the gate is separable, it can be trained. Supervised fine-tuning of a 3B model on 540 synthetic cases, predominantly dice, coins, jars and timers, drives commitment to 0.0% on the original cases and transfers to three unseen domains. It does not survive everything: the gate holds exactly when the response format leaves room to reason, and rigid formats that remove that room leave the model confident and wrong on questions it otherwise answers correctly. The gate is trainable and context-fragile, and deployment needs both halves of that sentence.

</details>


### [369] [What Makes Good Agentic Data? An ACE Lens on Data Generation for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27260)
*Xingshan Zeng,Zishan Xu,Boju Zhang,Yuzhou Wu,Lingzhi Wang,Jianghao Lin,Liangyou Li,Yasheng Wang,Lifeng Shang,Xin Jiang,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu,Qun Liu,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体日益依赖生成的交互数据这一趋势，提出一个两层分析框架来系统化“智能体数据生成”领域：首先将智能体数据表示为统一的因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)（环境规格、任务信号、交互实现、可选验证器），并按主要锚点与依赖结构组织生成范式；其次将生成形式化为受约束的分布设计，通过“准确性-复杂度-多样性”(ACE)视角进行刻画，用以解释文献中如何验证经验、构建与校准难度、扩展行为覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体数据生成工作横跨多个智能体领域，但以领域为中心的划分和异构的评测常常掩盖了共同的生成机制，并把候选构造与验证、选择混为一谈；此外，数据必须保持环境、任务、交互与成功信号之间的一致性，并产生有用而非仅为海量的经验。因此需要一个统一框架来归纳该领域。

Method: 提出两层框架：(1) 将智能体数据表示为公共因子化对象(E,q,τ,v)，包含环境规格、任务信号、交互实现和可选验证器，并按生成范式的“主要锚点”与“依赖结构”进行组织；(2) 将生成形式化为受约束的分布设计，即以Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE)透镜建模——准确性界定有依据且内部一致数据的可行支撑集，复杂度在此支撑集内依据声明学习者能力与执行配置分配学习质量，多样性控制数据覆盖度与冗余度。

Result: 借助该框架系统梳理了已有工作如何验证生成经验、构建并校准难度、扩展行为覆盖，揭示出文献整体转向执行接地(execution-grounded)的准确性、面向学习者(learner-relative)的复杂度，以及超越表面变化或数据集规模的多样性；并进一步讨论了ACE视角下关于扩展、数据源、训练范式与自适应学习的更广泛方向与新兴趋势。

Conclusion: 核心挑战不在于生成更多数据，而在于随着智能体与环境不断演化，持续分配有效、信息量大且不冗余的经验；即必须把数据生成视为随能力与环境动态变化的受约束分配问题。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on generated interaction data to learn how to interact with external environments. Agentic data generation must maintain consistency among environments, tasks, interactions, and success signals while producing experience that is useful rather than merely abundant. Existing work spans many agent domains, but domain-centered organization and heterogeneous evaluation often obscure common generation mechanisms and conflate candidate construction with verification and selection. This work develops a two-level framework for the field. First, we represent agentic data as a common factorized object $(E,q,τ,v)$, comprising an environment specification, task signal, interaction realization, and optional verifier. We organize generation paradigms by their primary anchor and dependency structure. Second, we formulate generation as constrained distribution design through the Accuracy-Complexity-divErsity (ACE) lens. Accuracy establishes the feasible support of grounded and internally consistent data. Within this support, Complexity places learning mass relative to the capability of a declared learner and execution configuration, while divErsity controls coverage and redundancy of data. Using this framework, we explore how prior work verifies generated experience, constructs and calibrates difficulty, and expands behavioral coverage. The literature reveals a shift toward execution-grounded accuracy, learner-relative complexity, and diversity beyond surface variation or dataset size. We further discuss broader directions and emerging trends in agentic data generation through the ACE lens, including their implications for scaling, data sources, training regimes and adaptive learning. Overall, the central challenge is not simply to generate more data, but to continually allocate valid, informative, and non-redundant experience as agents and environments evolve.

</details>


### [370] [Naive Prompt Optimization: Rethinking the Need for Complex Prompt Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27266)
*Yuan Chang,Xiaoqi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Naive Prompt Optimization（NPO），一种轻量级单谱系提示优化方法，仅通过教师模型迭代修订提示并结合回滚反馈，即可在更少的回滚次数下达到或超越复杂优化器GEPA的性能，并表明简单的线性提示优化可媲美更复杂的搜索过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示优化有望以较低的计算成本（优化与推理阶段）带来与微调模型权重相当的性能提升，是加速智能体递归自我改进（RSI）的重要途径。然而，近期提示优化器的发展日益倾向于不必要的复杂化，因此作者希望探究简单方法是否能达到同等甚至更优的效果。

Method: 提出NPO：一种轻量级单谱系（single-lineage）迭代式提示优化方法，由教师模型逐步修订提示，并利用回滚（rollout）反馈指导优化过程，不依赖复杂的搜索或群体机制。

Result: NPO在更少的回滚次数下取得与GEPA相当或更好的性能，且教师模型越强优势越明显，表明更强的教师推理能力可部分替代优化器侧的搜索复杂度；在交互式游戏中NPO总体与GEPA相当，而GRPO在部分不适合提示优化的任务上表现更好；NPO优化出的提示在逐字应用于其他学生模型时（尤其同族模型）也能带来类似的性能提升。

Conclusion: 初步结果表明简单、线性的提示优化流程可以与更复杂精密的搜索过程相媲美，提示优化器的设计应重视简洁性而非盲目的复杂度堆砌。

Abstract: Efficiently improving autonomous agents across diverse tasks is central to accelerating recursive self-improvement (RSI) in agentic AI, with prompt optimization emerging as a promising approach capable of delivering performance gains comparable to those achieved by fine-tuning model weights, while reducing computational costs in both optimization and serving. However, recent developments increasingly favor unnecessarily complex prompt optimizers. We introduce Naive Prompt Optimization (NPO), a lightweight single-lineage method that iteratively revises prompts using a teacher model with rollout feedback. NPO achieves comparable or better performance than GEPA with fewer rollouts, and its advantage increases with stronger teacher models, suggesting that stronger teacher reasoning can partially substitute for optimizer-side search complexity. In interactive games, NPO remains broadly competitive with GEPA, while GRPO performs better on some tasks less amenable to prompt optimization. We also show that NPO-optimized prompts elicit similar performance improvements when applied verbatim to other student models, especially across models within the same family. Overall, our preliminary results show that simple, linear prompt optimization can rival substantially more sophisticated and complex search procedures.

</details>


### [371] [BrailleBench: Investigating Multi-Criteria Braille Comprehension in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27268)
*Jinghan Zhang,Fengran Mo,Zhiyu Chen,Xiaoyan Han,Kunpeng Liu,Chang-Tien Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BrailleBench，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLMs）盲文理解能力的基准，涵盖英语与盲文 1 级、2 级下的数学、常识与多跳问答任务，并通过确定性、专家审核的流水线构建。对六个代表性 LLM 的评估显示，模型在印刷英语上的能力与盲文可及性之间存在持续差距，盲文理解与表达不对称，2 级盲文在输入侧尤为脆弱，全盲文请求会进一步降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs 已广泛用于知识获取与计算辅助，但其能力应同样惠及弱势群体。目前尚不清楚现有 AI 系统对盲人和聋盲用户是否足够包容，因为盲文的指示符、缩略形式及数字表示对模型理解提出了独特要求。为此需要系统性地评估 LLM 在盲文理解方面的表现。

Method: 作者提出 BrailleBench 基准，从五个数据集（涵盖数学、常识与多跳问答）中整理对齐 5,570 个实例，覆盖英语和盲文 1 级、2 级。基准设计了多种配置，分别考察模型理解盲文表述内容、用盲文表达答案以及完成端到端盲文交互的能力。为保证质量并避免评估偏差，基准通过自建 Braille Toolkit 的确定性、专家审核流水线构建，未使用任何由 LLM 生成的数据实例。作者从多个维度评估了六个代表性 LLM。

Result: 评估结果表明，模型在印刷英语能力与盲文可及性之间存在持续差距。盲文的理解与表达呈现不对称性：在输入侧，2 级盲文比 1 级盲文更为脆弱；而完全以盲文进行的请求会进一步降低模型性能。

Conclusion: 实验观察为未来盲文 AI 系统的开发提供了有价值的指导，BrailleBench 的全部相关资源已公开，供后续研究使用。

Abstract: Although Large language models (LLMs) mediate access to knowledge and computational assistance, their capabilities should benefit vulnerable groups in the same way. However, it is unclear whether existing AI systems are inclusive enough for blind and deafblind users to access the same functionality through Braille, whose indicators, contractions, and digital representations introduce distinct requirements for model comprehension. To this end, we introduce BrailleBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs in Braille comprehension from different Criteria. BrailleBench aligns 5,570 instances from five datasets, including mathematics, commonsense, and multi-hop question answering across English and Braille Grades 1 and 2. Different configurations are designed to understand whether the systems can comprehend Braille-authored content, express answers in Braille, and complete end-to-end Braille interaction. To ensure the quality and prevent evaluation bias, the benchmark is built through a deterministic, expert-reviewed pipeline via a self-created Braille Toolkit without using any data instances generated by LLMs. We evaluate six representative LLMs from various aspects. The results reveal a persistent gap between print-English capability and Braille accessibility. Braille understanding and expression are asymmetric, where Grade 2 is especially fragile on the input side compared to Grade 1, and fully Braille requests further reduce performance. The experimental observations provide valuable guidance for the development of future Braille AI systems. All related resources in BrailleBench are publicly available for future research.

</details>


### [372] [LLMs Can Design Near-Optimal OR Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27296)
*Jackie Baek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探究大型语言模型（LLM）能否为明确定义（well-specified）的运筹优化问题设计有效算法，覆盖库存控制、排队网络控制和品类优化三类问题。实验在两种层级上评估LLM：层级1为给定单个实例直接求解，层级2为仅给定问题类别描述与参数范围，输出能映射实例参数到解的通用算法。在几乎无需人工调优（单一未调优提示词+固定预算的Python沙箱）的条件下，最强模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎所有测试实例上达到或超越现有最优方法，且即使在层级2（算法在见到评估实例前已固定）依然成立。作者还观察到该能力在不到八个月发布的模型间显著提升，并据此认为前沿LLM可成为明确定义运筹问题算法设计的严肃经验基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM是否不仅仅能解决单个实例，还能自主设计针对明确定义运筹问题的通用算法，从而作为与专门方法竞争的经验基线。

Method: 针对库存控制、排队网络控制、品类优化三类问题，设计两种LLM使用层级：层级1给单实例求单解，层级2只给问题类别描述和参数范围、要求返回可映射参数到解的算法。人工参与极简：单一未调优提示词描述问题，模型在固定计算预算的Python沙箱工具中运行。

Result: 最强模型gpt-5.6-sol在几乎全部评估实例上匹配或优于现有最优方法；即使层级2（算法在评估实例出现前已固定）依然成立；且间隔不足八个月发布的新模型能力大幅提升。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的明确定义运筹问题，单次未调优的LLM查询即可生成与专门方法竞争甚至更优的算法，前沿LLM应被视为这类问题算法设计中的严肃经验基线，且该能力正快速进步。

Abstract: We ask whether large language models (LLMs) can design effective algorithms for well-specified operations research (OR) problems. We study inventory control, queueing network control, and assortment optimization. We evaluate two levels of LLM use: at level 1, the model receives one problem instance and returns a solution for that instance; at level 2, it receives only the problem class description and broad parameter ranges, and returns an algorithm that maps instance parameters to solutions. Human input is minimal: we give one untuned prompt that describes the problem, and the model has access to a Python sandbox tool with a fixed compute budget.
  The strongest model we test, gpt-5.6-sol, matches or outperforms the best existing method on almost all evaluated instances. This holds even at level 2, where the returned algorithm is fixed before seeing the evaluation instances. Performance also improves sharply across models released less than eight months apart, suggesting that this capability is moving quickly. Thus, for the well-specified operations problems we study, a single untuned LLM query can already produce algorithms competitive with specialized methods. These results suggest that frontier LLMs can be a serious empirical baseline for algorithm design in well-specified OR problems.

</details>


### [373] [Verify Smarter, Evolve Further: Efficient Harness Evolution through Behavior-Aware Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27311)
*Jinghan Xu,Yikai Zhang,Aili Chen,Weiyuan Li,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HarnessLens，一个预算感知的自动化 harness 演化框架。它联合探索任务空间与可配置组件，从执行轨迹中推导候选修改，并通过可归因证据门控（attributable-evidence gate）在行为相关任务上选择性验证每个候选。在三个 agent harness 和四个基准上，平均提升 7.6–13.6% 的留出性能，同时显著降低评估预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型 agent 的 harness（指令、工具、运行时组件）对行为影响重大，但对其适配需要代价高昂的验证。现有 propose-and-verify 方法通常在固定任务集上对每个候选打分，既在无关行为上浪费 rollout，又让聚合分数掩盖特定回归问题，导致验证效率与可靠性不佳。

Method: HarnessLens 是一个预算感知框架，包含三大机制：1）联合探索任务空间与用户可配置组件；2）从执行轨迹中推导候选修改；3）利用可归因证据门控，仅在与特定行为相关的任务上选择性验证每个候选，实现行为感知的定向验证。

Result: 在三个 agent harness 和四个基准上的实验表明，HarnessLens 平均将留出性能提升 7.6–13.6%，且相比竞争基线消耗显著更少的评估预算，展示了更好的样本效率。

Conclusion: 带有显式归因的行为感知验证能够在受限交互预算下实现更可靠、更样本高效的 harness 演化，表明将验证聚焦于相关行为而非固定任务集是提升自动化 agent 优化效率的关键。

Abstract: Agent harnesses shape how language-model agents use instructions, tools, and runtime components, but adapting these harnesses requires costly verification. Existing propose-and-verify methods typically score every candidate on a fixed task set, wasting rollouts on unrelated behaviors and allowing aggregate scores to obscure specific regressions. We introduce HarnessLens, a budget-aware framework for automated harness evolution. HarnessLens jointly explores the task space and user-configurable components, derives candidate modifications from execution trajectories, and selectively verifies each candidate on behavior-relevant tasks using an attributable-evidence gate. Across three agent harnesses and four benchmarks, HarnessLens improves average held-out performance by 7.6-13.6% while consuming substantially less evaluation budget than competing baselines. These results demonstrate that behavior-aware verification with explicit attribution enables more reliable and sample-efficient harness evolution under constrained interaction budgets. Our code is available at https://github.com/jhxu5214/HarnessLens.

</details>


### [374] [Not All Eval-Awareness Is Equal: Capabilities Framing Predicts Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27340)
*Allison Zhuang,Santiago Aranguri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示模型"评估意识"（eval-awareness）并非单一同质概念：其在思维链中的口头表达可被区分为能力倾向、安全倾向、两者兼具或两者皆非四种框架，且这些框架对模型遵从性的预测存在显著差异；聚合的抑制率指标可能掩盖安全性相关成分的真实变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估流程中常见的引导干预将评估意识视为一个需要抑制的单一数量，但该假设可能不成立。不同框架的评估意识对模型行为（如遵从性）的预测作用截然不同，因此有必要解构评估意识，避免聚合指标误导安全评估结论。

Method: 在Qwen3-32B模型与FORTRESS数据集上，作者将思维链中口头化的评估意识标注为能力倾向、安全倾向、两者兼具或两者皆非四类，比较各框架在多种引导条件下的遵从性预测差异；并通过针对评估意识阴性（eval-awareness-negative）样本的CoT-prefill干预实验检验因果性。

Result: 能力倾向框架比安全倾向框架的遵从性预测高出+24至+46个百分点（覆盖所有测试的引导条件）；CoT-prefill干预实验中10/11个prefill使遵从性按预测方向变化，支持因果联系；同时发现评估意识并非行为上均匀，聚合抑制率变化时安全性相关成分可能并未变化，相同"X%评估意识抑制率"可对应性质不同的行为结果。

Conclusion: 评估意识不是行为上统一的单一量，针对其的引导与抑制不能仅以聚合指标衡量；安全评估应区分评估意识的不同框架成分，否则聚合的抑制指标可能掩盖安全性相关成分的真实变化，导致误导性结论。

Abstract: Steering interventions targeting eval-awareness, a model's recognition that it is being tested, are increasingly used in safety evaluation pipelines, where evaluation-awareness is treated as a single quantity to be suppressed. We show that verbalized eval-awareness in chain-of-thought can be identified as capabilities-flavored ("the user is testing my ability to follow instructions"), safety-flavored ("the user is testing my boundaries"), both, or neither: framings that predict compliance very differently. On Qwen3-32B over the FORTRESS dataset, capabilities-framing predicts compliance with a +24 to +46 percentage-point gap over safety-framing across all tested steering conditions. A CoT-prefill intervention on eval-awareness-negative rollouts suggests the link is causal, with 10 of 11 prefills shifting compliance in the predicted direction. Then, eval-awareness is not behaviorally uniform: aggregate suppression rates can move while the safety-relevant component does not, and the same "X% suppression of eval-awareness" can correspond to qualitatively different behavioral outcomes.

</details>


### [375] [Sophistication in GenAI Use: Field Evidence from a Large Firm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27364)
*Nicholas J. Hallman,Zachary T. Kowaleski,Anu Puvvada,Jaime J. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用某大型企业近4000名后台员工在2025年八个月内的713,564条提示词及其大语言模型响应数据，刻画了生成式AI使用的成熟度（sophistication）差异：资深员工使用更成熟、不同职能部门差异显著（战略、数字创新、项目管理最高），且成熟度既不随时间自然提升、也不因正式AI培训产生持久改善，表明成熟使用难以改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在组织中快速普及，但企业对员工“如何使用”AI的成熟程度缺乏系统度量与经验证据。作者希望回答：后台员工在genAI使用成熟度上存在哪些差异、由什么因素驱动、以及能否被干预改善，从而为管理者优化AI应用效果和研究者开展后续研究提供基础。

Method: 基于某大型企业的专有数据开展实证研究，观测2025年八个月内近4000名后台员工、覆盖15个职能部门共713,564条员工提示词及其对应的大语言模型响应；通过编码或量化提示词的复杂度、任务类型等维度构建genAI使用成熟度指标，并对员工资历、职能部门以及时间与AI培训干预进行对比分析。

Result: 三项主要发现：其一，资深员工的genAI使用成熟度更高，说明领域专长与genAI能力存在互补效应；其二，成熟度在职能间差异显著，以战略、数字创新和项目管理三个聚焦公司级战略举措与组织变革的部门最高；其三，成熟度随时间没有提升，正式的AI培训也未带来持久改善，提示成熟使用行为难以改变。

Conclusion: 本研究为组织中的genAI使用成熟度提供了可操作的度量工具和实证洞见：管理者可据此识别高成熟度示范部门与员工、调整培训策略以改善AI应用成效，研究人员也可在此基础上进一步探索影响AI使用能力的机制与干预路径。

Abstract: We study how sophistication in generative AI (genAI) use varies among the back-office workforce of a large firm. Using proprietary data, we observe 713,564 employee prompts and their corresponding large language model responses from nearly 4,000 back-office employees across 15 functional areas over eight months in 2025. We document three main findings. First, senior employees exhibit more sophisticated genAI use, consistent with domain expertise complementing genAI capabilities. Second, sophistication varies considerably across functions and is highest in Strategy, Digital Innovation, and Project Management, three groups that share a focus on firmwide strategic initiatives and organizational change. Third, we observe neither improvements in sophistication over time nor lasting improvements following formal AI training, suggesting that sophisticated use can be difficult to change. Together, our study provides measures of and insights into sophisticated genAI use that managers can use to improve outcomes and that researchers can use in future research.

</details>


### [376] [CorporateBench: Large-Scale Q&A Benchmarking with Temporal Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27391)
*Sil Hamilton,Albert Yu Sun,Oscar J. Romero,Carl-Leander Henneking,David Mimno,Bishan Yang,Igor Labutov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CorporateBench（CB），一个基于人类验证的、面向企业级文档规模的LLM多任务问答基准，评测语料超过23万份文档，覆盖信息抽取与知识库查询两个维度，并揭示LLM在输入规模接近真实场景时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越擅长回答企业级文档集合的复杂问题，但评测存在困难：企业不愿共享内部通信数据，而现有合成数据集过于简单，无法逼近真实企业通信网络中的规模与复杂度，评测生态存在关键缺口。

Method: 构建CorporateBench（CB）：基于随时间演化的、描述一致世界的知识库进行采样，生成4个规模从12到10,000名员工的合成企业，每个企业的评测语料超过230,000份文档，并通过人类验证确保跨文档逻辑一致性。基准覆盖信息抽取与知识库查询两个维度，并用于评测5个LLM。

Result: 评测结果表明，当输入规模接近现实规模时，5个LLM的性能均明显下降，表明现有模型在企业级文档规模下的推理能力不足。

Conclusion: CB为LLM开发者提供了面向企业通信推理的评测指标，填补了大规模、逻辑一致且贴近真实场景的基准测试空白，有助于推动LLM在企业级文档理解方面的改进。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly able to answer complex questions about enterprise-scale document collections. But evaluation is hard: companies don't want to share internal communications, and synthetic datasets have been overly simple. We present CorporateBench (CB), a human-validated multi-task Q&A benchmark whose scale approaches the conditions LLMs encounter in corporate communication networks, with evaluation corpora surpassing 230,000 documents. CB evaluates LLMs across two dimensions (information extraction and knowledge base querying) through four synthetically generated firms ranging from 12 to 10,000 employees. Each corpus is sampled from a temporally evolving knowledge base describing a consistent world, guaranteeing cross-document logical consistency even across hundreds of thousands of documents. We evaluate five LLMs on CB, revealing increasingly poor performance as input size approaches realistic scales. CB provides LLM developers a metric for corporate communication reasoning, filling a crucial gap in the benchmarking ecosystem.

</details>


### [377] [Learning a Continuous Sepsis Severity Score Without Hour-by-Hour Supervision: A Two-Site Retrospective Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27421)
*Kevin Zhu,Ryan Zhang,Baraa Abed,Tilendra Choudhary,Malvern Madondo,Mehak Arora,Yixuan Yang,Alasdair Gent,Aditya Nagori,Omer T. Inan,Krista L. Haines,Patrick Georgoff,Suresh M. Agarwal,Vijay Krishnamoorthy,Tetsu Ohnuma,Mihai V. Podgoreanu,Michael R. Pinsky,Gilles Clermont,Craig M. Coopersmith,Craig S. Jabaley,Rishikesan Kamaleswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用两个医院系统共36,807名符合Sepsis-3标准的成人患者数据，开发了一种基于43项常规记录变量、覆盖72小时治疗窗口的脓毒症指数。与传统固定权重评分不同，该方法将死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号（而非单时间节点的状态标签），允许信用非均匀地在时间步之间重新分配。结果显示非幸存者得分比幸存者高1.19-1.64分（0-10分制），指数随时间变化与乳酸变化相关（rho=0.39），跨机构泛化表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前临床使用的脓毒症严重程度指数依赖数十年前建立的固定变量与权重，这些指数离散化粗糙，且校准对象已无法反映当代重症监护的实际情况。目前尚无直接从患者轨迹中学习的替代方法进入常规临床应用，存在开发基于数据驱动的新型脓毒症指数的迫切需求。

Method: 开展了一项回顾性双队列研究，纳入马萨诸塞州和佐治亚州两个医院系统分别29,116例和7,691例符合Sepsis-3标准的成人患者。使用43项常规记录变量，覆盖72小时治疗窗口。关键创新在于将死亡率作为治疗层面的排序信号（而非每个状态的目标），使信用能够在时间步之间非均匀地重新分配。采用永久保留的20%测试集进行评估，使用临床小故事和Spearman相关性，并通过自助重采样整个患者获得不确定性区间。

Result: 在该排序方案下，非幸存者在0-10分制上比幸存者高出1.19-1.64分（覆盖所有基线SOFA-2分层），在乳酸、平均动脉压（MAP）和肌酐分层内结果相似。指数随患者的变化与乳酸变化相关（Spearman rho=0.39，n=1,854），与MAP和肌酐的相关性较弱。队列层面，不同机构训练的模型之间的交叉机构一致性为同机构相关性的70-77%；外部患者内相关性为0.54和0.59，对应上限0.92和0.90。该指数与已有指数相关，而零对照保持在接近零的水平。

Conclusion: 该指数提供了每小时级的预后信息，能有效区分患者结局并与临床预期一致，表明其作为辅助临床判断的决策支持工具有一定的潜力。同时指出了需要进一步验证其临床应用价值的空间。

Abstract: Currently used sepsis severity indices rely on fixed variables and weights established decades ago, which are coarsely discretized and calibrated to a cohort that no longer reflects contemporary critical care. No alternative learned directly from patient trajectories is in routine use. We conducted a retrospective two-cohort study on a total of 29,116 and 7,691 adult patients meeting Sepsis-3 criteria from two hospital systems in Massachusetts and Georgie, respectively.
  We developed a sepsis index using 43 routinely charted variables over a 72-hour treatment window. Unlike previous studies, we use mortality as a treatment-level ranking signal rather than a per-state target, allowing credit to be redistributed non-uniformly across timesteps. Evaluation was done on a permanent 20% test holdout, using clinical vignettes and Spearman correlation. Uncertainty intervals were obtained by bootstrap resampling of whole patients. Under this ranking scheme, non-survivors scored 1.19-1.64 points higher than survivors on a 0-10 scale within all strata of baseline SOFA-2, with similar results stratifying within lactate, mean arterial pressure (MAP), and creatinine. Within-patient change in the index correlated with change in lactate (Spearman rho = 0.39; n = 1,854). Similar, weaker correlations were found for MAP and creatinine. On a cohort level, cross-institutional agreement measured by Spearman correlation between models trained on different sites, were 70-77% of same-site correlation. External within-patient correlations were 0.54 and 0.59 against ceilings of 0.92 and 0.90. Our index also correlated with established indices, while null controls stayed near zero.
  Our index demonstrated hourly prognostic information that meaningfully separates patient outcomes and is consistent with clinical expectation, indicating potential as a decision support tool complementing clinical judgement.

</details>


### [378] [Mechanistic Reaction Prediction via Discrete Flow Matching on Graph-Structured Electron Occupation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27429)
*Nguyen Xuan-Vu,Octavian Susanu,Daniel Armstrong,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAELLE，将化学反应建模为电子占据向量上的离散流匹配（而非传统的产物生成或图编辑），通过连续时间马尔可夫链在键合、非键合和氢位点上描述反应，并利用最优传输构造可解释的机理编辑轨迹，在USPTO-480K基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时在结构复杂度和反应类型两类分布外场景下保持稳健，并能恢复符合化学机理的轨迹和预测副产物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学反应本质上是电子空间的变换，但现有机器学习方法要么通过从头生成产物分子，要么在分子拓扑上进行启发式图编辑，均未直接建模电子重排过程，缺乏机理可解释性。

Method: MAELLE将反应物到产物的映射建模为定义在键合、非键合和氢位点上图结构整数电子占据空间上的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）；通过最优传输将离散流匹配混合路径推广到离散电子重排上，无需基元步骤标注即可构造机理可解释的中间编辑轨迹序列。

Result: MAELLE在USPTO-480K基准上与领先反应预测模型性能相当；在结构复杂度和反应类型两类分布外设置下，当现有方法性能下降时MAELLE仍保持较强表现；此外能恢复与已知化学相符的机理性轨迹并预测副产物。

Conclusion: 直接在电子占据空间上建模反应流匹配，不仅获得了具有竞争力的预测精度和更强的分布外稳健性，还天然具备机理可解释性与副产物预测能力，为反应预测提供了新的建模范式。

Abstract: Chemical reactions are fundamentally transformations in electron space, yet most machine learning approaches model them either through \textit{de novo} generation of product molecules or through heuristic graph edits that operate directly on molecular topology.
  We introduce MAELLE (\textbf{M}ech\textbf{A}nistic \textbf{E}dit f\textbf{L}ow-matching on e\textbf{L}ectron r\textbf{E}arrangements), which instead models reactions as discrete flow matching over electron occupation vectors.
  Concretely, we formulate the reactant-to-product mapping as a Continuous-time Markov Chain (CTMC) over the graph-structured integer-valued electron occupation space defined on all bonding, non-bonding, and hydrogen sites.
  To construct the intermediate edit trajectories, we generalize the discrete flow matching mixture path to discrete electron rearrangements using Optimal Transport, yielding a sequence of mechanistically interpretable edit moves without requiring elementary step annotations.
  MAELLE achieves competitive performance on the USPTO-480K benchmark compared with leading reaction prediction models.
  Beyond in-distribution accuracy, we evaluate robustness across two out-of-distribution settings - structural complexity and reaction type - and find that MAELLE maintains strong performance where existing methods degrade.
  Finally, because the learned flow operates over the full electron redistribution, MAELLE naturally recovers mechanistic trajectories that align with known chemistry and can predict side products of a reaction.

</details>


### [379] [WikiSkill: Compiling Agent Experience into Persistent Knowledge for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27454)
*Liyan Tang,Cyrus Rashtchian,Chun-Sung Ferng,Andrew Tomkins,Da-Cheng Juan,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 WikiSkill 框架，通过将智能体的技能与持久化知识库（wiki）协同进化，把分散在优化历史中的洞察系统性地沉淀到 wiki 中，使后续技能更新可在此基础上构建，从而在多个基准和模型上持续超越现有技能进化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽然能从智能体经验中自动发现技能以支持逐步适应，但指导技能开发的洞察通常散落在优化历史中，缺乏跨迭代的系统性复用，限制了技能进化的效率和效果。

Method: WikiSkill 框架将原始执行经验、累积知识和可执行技能三者分离，并持续将经验整合（consolidation）进持久化的知识库 wiki 中；后续的技能更新（skill updates）以 wiki 中沉淀的知识为基础进行构建与进化，形成经验→知识→技能的不断迭代循环。

Result: 在多样化的基准和模型上，WikiSkill 一致优于最先进的技能进化方法，且在多数模型-基准组合上超过无技能基线。技能进化与模型规模互补：更大模型从进化技能中获益更多，而带技能的小模型可超越不带技能的更大模型；进化技能可在模型及模型家族间有效迁移，且其他模型进化的技能可优于自进化技能。消融实验证实 wiki 中的持久知识累积对技能进化至关重要。

Conclusion: 系统性累积与精炼智能体经验，是开发可复用、可迁移技能的有效途径；持久知识库作为技能进化的记忆中枢，显著提升了智能体技能进化与迁移的整体效果。

Abstract: Agent skills package specialized knowledge and workflows into reusable resources that extend AI agent capabilities. Recent work automatically discovers such skills from agent experience, which enables agents to progressively adapt through interaction. However, the insights that guide skill development typically remain scattered across optimization histories, limiting their systematic reuse across iterations. We introduce WikiSkill, a framework that co-evolves agent skills with a persistent knowledge base (wiki). At a high level, WikiSkill separates raw execution experience, accumulated knowledge, and executable skills, while continuously consolidating experience into the wiki, which subsequent skill updates can build on. Across diverse benchmarks and models, WikiSkill consistently outperforms state-of-the-art skill-evolution methods and improves over no-skill baselines in most model-benchmark settings. We find that skill evolution complements model scaling: larger models generally benefit more from evolved skills, while smaller models with skills can outperform substantially larger models without them. We also find that evolved skills transfer effectively across models and model families, and skills evolved by other models can outperform self-evolved skills. Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [380] [Comparative Evaluation of 3D Reconstruction Methods for Immersive Visualization of Laboratory Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27301)
*Brian De La Cruz,Aaron Y. Zhao,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Sawyer J. Lazar,Xianming Tan,Daniel Szafir,David S. Lawrence*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本研究比较了摄影测量、NeRF、高斯泼溅和LiDAR四种3D重建方法在制作实验室物品全息教学模型方面的效果，发现NeRF方法在透明、反光或低纹理物品上表现最稳定，且形状和颜色的还原优于纹理，为AR/MR沉浸式教育提供了可行的工作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨当前3D重建方法能否为教育用途创建逼真的实验室物品全息表征，并识别各方法的优势与局限，以支持沉浸式学习环境的开发。

Method: 采用四种重建方法（摄影测量、基于NeRF的方法、高斯泼溅、LiDAR）生成了常见实验室物品的全息模型，并由研究生采用重复测量设计对模型的形状、颜色、纹理和视觉缺陷进行保真度评估。

Result: NeRF方法在各类物品中产生了最一致的高保真表征，尤其在透明、反光或低纹理物品上表现突出；形状和颜色的还原总体优于纹理，说明部分视觉属性在全息教育模型中仍难以准确呈现。

Conclusion: 研究不仅明确了各重建方法的优劣，还展示了可用于课前准备、空间推理和学生参与的沉浸式学习对象创建流程，为教育者和研究者在AR/MR环境下设计数字学习体验提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: In this study, we examined whether current 3D reconstruction methods can support the creation of realistic holographic representations of laboratory objects for educational use. In this regard, we compared four approaches: photogrammetry, a neural radiance field (NeRF)-based method, Gaussian splatting, and LiDAR. These methods were used to generate holographic models of common laboratory items and their fidelity was evaluated by graduate students. Participants assessed the models for shape, color, texture, and visual defects using a repeated-measures design. Across objects, the NeRF-based method produced the most consistently high-fidelity representations, particularly for transparent, reflective, or low-texture items that were difficult to capture with other approaches. Shape and color were generally reproduced more successfully than texture, suggesting that some visual properties remain more challenging to represent accurately in educational holograms. Beyond identifying the strengths and limitations of each reconstruction method, the study demonstrates a practical workflow for creating immersive learning objects that may support pre-laboratory preparation, spatial reasoning, and student engagement in AR/MR-based educational environments. These findings offer design-relevant insights for educators and researchers developing immersive digital learning experiences.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [381] [Report of the 2026 Workshop on Next-Generation Ecosystems for Scientific Computing: Harnessing Community, Software, and AI for Cross-Disciplinary Team Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26519)
*Lois Curfman McInnes,Dorian Arnold,Prasanna Balaprakash,Mike Bernhardt,Franck Cappello,Beth Cerny,Deborah DiazGranados,Anshu Dubey,Nichole Etienne,Roscoe Giles,Diego Gomez-Zara,Denice Ward Hood,Mary Ann Leung,Vanessa Lopez-Marrero,Olivia B. Newton,Irene Qualters,Keita Teranishi,Stefan M. Wild,Gabrielle Allen,Richard Arthur,Alexandra Ballow,Tony Baylis,David E. Bernholdt,Daniel Bielich,Johanna Cohoon,Jeremy Crampton,Charles Ferenbaugh,Stephen M. Fiore,Thomas Herault,Tanzima Islam,Stephen Jacobsohn,Meifeng Lin,Charles Lively,Satoshi Matsuoka,Stasa Milojevic,Daniel Nichols,Chris Oehmen,Santiago Ospina Tabares,Michael E. Papka,Katherine Riley,Damian Rouson,Sudip K. Seal,Brittany Segundo,John Shalf,Andrew Siegel,Valerie Taylor,Jim Willenbring,Lou Woodley*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文是一份关于2026年“下一代科学计算生态系统”研讨会的报告摘要，总结了AI、异构计算、自动化与数据密集型研究对科学计算的深刻影响，提出了四个相互依存的战略主题（AI驱动的科学发现软件生态、信任/验证/可追溯性、人机协作与范式转变、劳动力/教学/治理），并将其转化为八项社区行动优先事项，旨在构建可信、可持续、创新且有韧性的科学计算生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学计算正经历快速变革，AI进展、异构计算、自动化和数据密集型研究不仅重塑了计算工具，也改变了支撑科学发现的机构、劳动力模式和协作实践。为应对这些变化、通过社会-技术协同设计强化科学计算生态系统，需要系统性地综合社区意见并明确优先行动方向。

Method: 该报告基于2026年“下一代科学计算生态系统”研讨会（一个为期三年的系列研讨会的第二届）的讨论综合而成，采用社会-技术协同设计（socio-technical co-design）的分析框架，围绕讨论中涌现的四个相互依存的战略主题进行归纳，并将其翻译转化为八项面向社区的优先行动事项。

Result: 研讨会确定了四个相互依存的战略主题：AI驱动的科学发现软件生态系统、信任/验证/可追溯性、人机协作与范式转变、劳动力/教学/治理；并据此提出八项社区行动优先事项，涵盖共享研究基础设施、信任与可追溯性、用户体验、人机协作、劳动力发展、跨部门协调、管理与可持续性，以及科学价值评估。

Conclusion: 这些优先事项为构建科学计算生态系统指明了方向，使系统能够在AI在科学工作中作用日益增强的背景下，保持可信、可持续、创新和有韧性。

Abstract: Scientific computing is undergoing rapid transformation as advances in artificial intelligence, heterogeneous computing, automation, and data-intensive research reshape not only computational tools but also the institutions, workforce models, and collaborative practices that support scientific discovery. This report synthesizes insights from the 2026 Workshop on Next-Generation Ecosystems for Scientific Computing, the second in a three-year series focused on strengthening scientific computing ecosystems through socio-technical co-design. Workshop discussions identified four interdependent strategic themes: software ecosystems for AI-enabled scientific discovery; trust, validation, and traceability; human-AI teaming and paradigm shifts; and workforce, pedagogy, and governance. The report translates these themes into eight priorities for community action spanning shared research infrastructure, trust and traceability, user experience, human-AI teaming, workforce development, cross-sector coordination, stewardship and sustainability, and evaluation of scientific value. Together, these priorities outline directions for building scientific computing ecosystems that remain trustworthy, sustainable, innovative, and resilient as AI assumes a growing role in scientific work.

</details>


<div id='cs.GT'></div>

# cs.GT [[Back]](#toc)

### [382] [Simultaneous Envy and Equitability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26410)
*Hadi Hosseini,Shraddha Pathak,Lirong Xia,Chengkai Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分物品与杂务公平分配中两大根本公平概念——嫉妒自由（EF）与均等性（EQ）的兼容性，系统考察其松弛形式（EF1/EQX、EQ1/EQX等）的存在性与计算复杂性，发现两者之间鲜明的对比：归一化可加估值下EF1+EQ1甚至可能不存在，但归一化二元物品在至多七位代理人时可由算法求得；而二元杂务则对任意代理人数量都保证更强的EFX+EQX，且无需归一化。此外，文章首次研究跨概念的ex-ante-ex-post混合保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有公平分配工作要么关注多个紧密相关公平概念的同时满足，要么专注于在ex-ante与ex-post世界之间实现单一概念。作者希望探讨两类根本不同的公平概念——嫉妒自由（EF）与均等性（EQ）之间的兼容性，并填补跨概念混合保证（一个概念在前验、另一个概念在后验）这一研究空白。

Method: 作者针对不可分物品和不可分杂务两种场景，分别研究EF1+EQ1与EFX+EQX等松弛组合的存在性及求解复杂性；给出归一化可加估值下EF1+EQ1可能不存在的反例，并为归一化二元物品（至多七位代理人）设计计算EF1+EQ1分配的算法；对二元杂务给出更强的EFX+EQX保证；同时提出并形式化跨概念的ex-ante-ex-post混合公平保证问题。

Result: （1）即使估值归一化且可加，EF1+EQ1分配也可能不存在；（2）归一化二元物品在代理人数量至多七人时，可计算得到EF1+EQ1分配；（3）与之形成鲜明对比，二元杂务在任意代理人数量、甚至无需归一化的条件下都保证更强的EFX+EQX；（4）首创性地建立了跨概念的ex-ante-ex-post保证框架。

Conclusion: 嫉妒自由与均等性这两类根本不同的公平概念在不可分物品与杂务场景下的兼容性存在显著差异：物品场景下EF1+EQ1稀缺且计算受限，而杂务场景却拥有更强的无条件EFX+EQX保证。该对比凸显了两类场景在公平分配结构上的根本区别，并为未来在跨概念混合公平保证方向的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recent work in fair division has focused on either simultaneously satisfying closely related fairness notions or achieving a single notion across the ex-ante and ex-post worlds. We study the compatibility of two fundamentally different fairness notions: envy-freeness and equitability. For indivisible goods-only and chores-only settings, we study the existence and complexity of simultaneously satisfying their relaxations, revealing sharp contrasts between the two settings. We show that EF1+EQ1 may fail to exist even for normalized, additive valuations. Our main algorithmic result computes an EF1+EQ1 allocation for normalized binary goods with at most seven agents. In sharp contrast, binary chores admit the stronger EFX+EQX guarantee for any number of agents, even without normalization. We further initiate the study of cross-notion ex-ante--ex-post guarantees, asking whether randomized allocations can provide ex-ante guarantees for one notion while preserving ex-post guarantees for another.

</details>


### [383] [Trusting AI in Competitive Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26539)
*Jussi Keppo,Yuze Li,Gerry Tsoukalas,Nuo Yuan*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 通过实验室实验（273名卖家、91个三人市场、30轮），研究寡头定价中人们使用AI建议时信任程度的分化。结果表明市场性别构成塑造了卖家对AI建议的学习方式：全女性市场中AI定价建议使价格提高29%、利润提高39%，而全男性和混合性别市场无显著效果。非齐次隐马尔可夫模型揭示，盈利轮次预测全女性市场对AI的遵从上升、其他市场遵从下降，与"习得信任"和"自利归因"一致，且反转了近期关于性别与AI的既有证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中人们对AI建议的信任在使用过程中出现分化——有人越来越信任、有人越来越不信任。作者希望在寡头定价这一"建议无法自我证明"（因结果取决于竞争对手反应）的背景下，研究这种信任分化及其驱动因素，特别是市场性别构成的作用。

Method: 实验室实验：273名卖家在91个三人市场中竞争30轮，操纵是否存在AI定价建议以及市场性别构成（全女性、全男性、混合）。使用非齐次隐马尔可夫模型（Non-Homogeneous Hidden Markov Model）分析建议遵从度随时间与市场构成的动态变化。

Result: 全女性市场中AI建议使价格提高29%、利润提高39%；全男性和混合性别市场无显著效果。隐马尔可夫模型显示构成特异性的动态关联：在盈利轮次后，全女性市场对AI的遵从上升，而其他市场遵从下降，这符合"习得信任"与"自利归因"的机制解释。

Conclusion: 市场性别构成会显著调节人类对AI建议的学习与采纳，进而影响AI实际的经济效应。平台治理和监管监督不能只聚焦于算法本身，还必须关注塑造算法效果的人类因素（此处为市场参与者的构成与心理归因机制）。

Abstract: Problem definition: People's trust in AI advice diverges as they use it, deepening for some and eroding for others. We study this divergence in oligopoly pricing, where advice cannot prove itself: rivals' responses decide whether it pays off. Methodology/results: In a laboratory experiment, 273 sellers compete across 91 three-seller markets over 30 rounds; we vary the presence of AI pricing recommendations and the gender composition of the market (female-only, male-only, or mixed). We find that the gender composition of the market shapes how sellers learn from the advice, and where prices settle as a result. In female-only markets, recommendations raise prices by 29% and profits by 39%; in male-only and mixed-gender markets, they have no significant effect. A Non-Homogeneous Hidden Markov Model reveals a composition-specific dynamic association: profitable rounds predict rising adherence to the AI in female-only markets and declining adherence otherwise, a pattern consistent with learned trust and self-serving attribution. The pattern reverses what recent evidence on gender and AI would predict. Managerial implications: We discuss implications for platform governance and regulatory oversight, which should focus not only on the algorithm but on the human side that shapes its effects.

</details>


### [384] [A lone divider allocation algorithm with subjective divisibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26801)
*Uriel Feige*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究主观可分性分配模型中的最大最小份额（MMS）分配问题，将该模型中已有的 5/9-MMS 分配存在性结果改进为 3/5-MMS 分配存在性，证明基于对孤分隔离（lone divider）框架的改编。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主观可分性分配模型中，商品可能是完全可分的（价值加性累加），也可能只能在整体上被持有（小于 1 的任何分数价值为 0），该模型混合了可分割与不可分割商品。此前工作仅证明 5/9-MMS 分配存在，比率仍有提升空间，因此需要更强的近似 MMS 保证。

Method: 作者将孤分隔离（lone divider）框架进行改编，用于构造分配过程，从而证明在所有代理的 valuations 均为非负可加、且部分商品呈“全有或全无”价值结构的前提下，总能得到 3/5-MMS 的分配方案。

Result: 证明在该主观可分性模型中 3/5-MMS 分配总是存在，将此前已知的 5/9（约 0.556）逼近比提升至 3/5（0.6）。

Conclusion: 通过对孤分隔离方法的适配，本文改进了主观可分性下 MMS 分配的存在性下界，为该类混合可分性模型的公平分配研究提供了更紧的结果。

Abstract: In the subjective divisibility allocation model, all goods are divisible, every agent $i$ has a non-negative additive valuation function $v_i$, where for some goods $v_i$ is additive over fractions of the good, and for some goods, any fraction smaller than~1 has value~0. Previous work established the existence of $\frac{5}{9}$-MMS allocations in this setting. We show that $\frac{3}{5}$-MMS allocations exist. Our proof is based on an adaptation of the lone divider framework.

</details>


### [385] [Robust Lottery Compression for Metric Voting: A Transfer Principle for Bounded Randomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26854)
*Jianhao Jia,Bo Peng*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究有界随机性（Bounded Randomness）下投票规则的度量失真（Metric Distortion）：规则需确定性地选取 K 个候选人的多重集，再从中均匀随机抽取。本文证明该受限模型可逼近当前不受限随机化下最优上界 5/2，具体地，对任意 K≥802 存在失真至多 5/2 + 3(π/(8K))^(1/3) + 2√(π/(8K)) 的规则，即 O(ε^(-3)) 个条目即可实现 5/2+ε 失真，与选民/候选人人数无关；并证明 164 个条目即可严格低于确定性失真界 3，给出打破确定性壁垒所需最小表长 N* 满足 2≤N*≤164。核心贡献是维数无关（dimension-free）压缩定理，并通过对 Mixed Integrated Veto 提前消除被淘汰候选来满足支撑条件，综合修复代价与压缩代价得到 O(K^(-1/3)) 收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的度量失真研究显示，确定性投票规则下最优失真下界为 3，而完全不受限的随机化规则可达到更低的失真上界 5/2。但在实际场景中，完全随机化投票机制难以实现或不可取，因此 Motivated 是考察"受限随机化"模型：规则需先确定性选出一个包含 K 个候选人的多重集，再等概率从中抽取。先前工作表明该受限模型已能略优于确定性最优界 3，本文旨在探究该模型究竟能多接近不受限随机化的 5/2 基准，并刻画达到 5/2+ε 所需的最小条目数以及打破确定性壁垒所需的最小 K。

Method: 核心是提出一个维数无关的压缩定理：若某个彩票（lottery）的失真至多为 ρ，且其支撑中每个候选人的确定性失真至多为 H，则该彩票可被一个 K 条目的均匀近似彩票替代，失真至多 ρ+(H+1)√(π/(8K))。这意味着"可能结果本身已很规整"的彩票只需付出 O(K^(-1/2)) 的压缩损失。由于 Mixed Integrated Veto 不满足这一支撑条件，作者先移除早期即被淘汰的结果，以 O(τ²) 的失真代价换取支撑内每个候选人的确定性失真为 O(1/τ) 的保证；再在修复代价与压缩代价之间作权衡，从而得到 O(K^(-1/3)) 的收敛速率。

Result: 对每个整数 K≥802，存在有界随机性规则，其失真至多为 5/2 + 3(π/(8K))^(1/3) + 2√(π/(8K))；由此得出 O(ε^(-3)) 个条目即可实现失真 5/2+ε，且该条目数与选民数和候选人数量无关。此外，164 个条目已经能使失真严格低于 3，从而得到最小表长 N* 满足 2≤N*≤164。

Conclusion: 有界随机性模型虽然只要求"先确定性选 K 个候选、再均匀随机抽取"这种极弱的随机化，却几乎能达到完全不受限随机化下的最优失真基准 5/2。压缩定理表明只要支撑内候选人本身确定性表现良好，均匀 K-条目近似仅带来 O(K^(-1/2)) 的失真损失；通过修复 Mixed Integrated Veto 的支撑条件并权衡修复与压缩成本，最终实现 O(K^(-1/3)) 的收敛率。该工作把打破确定性失真壁垒所需的最小表长压缩到 164，并留出 2≤N*≤164 的开放区间待后续改进。

Abstract: We study metric distortion in randomized social choice under bounded randomness: on every preference profile, the voting rule must deterministically identify a multiset of $K$ candidates and then select a uniformly random entry. Previous work showed that this restricted model can beat the optimal deterministic distortion of $3$. We show that it can in fact approach the current best unrestricted upper benchmark of $5/2$. For every integer $K\ge 802$, there exists a bounded-randomness rule with distortion at most $\frac{5}{2} +3\left(\fracπ{8K}\right)^{1/3} +2\sqrt{\fracπ{8K}}$.
  Consequently, $O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ entries suffice for distortion $5/2+\varepsilon$, independently of the numbers of voters and candidates. We also show that $164$ entries already achieve distortion strictly below $3$, giving $2\le N^\star\le 164$ for the minimum list size needed to break the deterministic barrier. Our main technical contribution is a dimension-free compression theorem: if a lottery has distortion at most $ρ$ and every candidate in its support has deterministic distortion at most $H$, then it admits a uniform $K$-entry approximation with distortion at most $ρ+(H+1)\sqrt{π/(8K)}$. Thus, lotteries whose possible outcomes are already well behaved incur only $O(K^{-1/2})$ compression loss. Mixed Integrated Veto does not satisfy this support condition, so we first remove early-eliminated outcomes, trading $O(τ^2)$ distortion loss for an $O(1/τ)$ bound on the deterministic distortion of every supported candidate. Balancing this repair cost against compression yields the $O(K^{-1/3})$ convergence rate.

</details>


### [386] [Blindfolded pursuit with delays of your choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27347)
*Torben Schürenberg,Maximilian J. Stahlberg*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究单追捕者对不可见逃跑者的图上的追逃博弈。追捕者可以给图的边赋予整数旅行时间并指定一个查询顶点序列，逃跑者则选择一条游走来躲避所有查询。文中证明：在赋予旅行时间的能力下，单一追捕者可以在任意图上以多项式时间成功捕获逃跑者；若逃跑者还可提前未知时刻出发，追捕者仍可在指数时间内获胜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典追逃博弈（如猎人-兔子博弈和消防博弈）在无权图上通常需要多个追捕者（猎人/消防员），其数量可随顶点数线性增长。作者试图探究：赋予单一追捕者选择边旅行时间的能力，能否弥补人数上的劣势，使单个追捕者即可在任意图上成功捕获不可见的逃跑者。

Method: 将两类经典设置形式化建模：（1）单位旅行时间且逃跑者每步必须移动，对应于猎人-兔子博弈；（2）单位旅行时间且逃跑者可以等待，可表述为消防博弈。作者考察追捕者自由指定边旅行时间这一额外自由度，并分析其带来的算法与复杂性影响；此外还考虑逃跑者可在追捕者未知的较早时刻出发的变体。

Result: 在赋予旅行时间选择权后，单一追捕者可在任意图上以多项式时间完成搜索（包含逃跑者必须移动及可等待两种设置）；这与无权图情形形成鲜明对比——后者所需猎人/消防员数量可随顶点数线性增长。若逃跑者还可提前未知时刻出发，追捕者仍能获胜，但所需时间是指数级的。

Conclusion: 赋予追捕者指定边旅行时间的能力可以显著改变追逃博弈的复杂性：它使单个追捕者即可在任意图上多项式时间内获胜，克服了无权图中追捕者数量需求随图规模线性增长的困难。然而，引入未知的提前出发时间会使问题本质上更难，追捕者的成功时间退化为指数级。

Abstract: We study pursuit-evasion games on graphs with a single pursuer and an invisible evader. The pursuer may assign integer travel times to the edges of the graph and specify a finite sequence of vertices to query, one per time step. The evader then chooses a walk over the same time horizon, aiming to elude all queries. With unit travel times, the setting in which the evader must move at every time step is known as the hunter and rabbit game, while the variant in which the evader can wait at a vertex can be phrased as a firefighting game: the vertices of a burning graph must be extinguished, and any vertex left burning reignites its neighbors. For both settings, we show that the power to choose travel times allows a single pursuer to succeed in polynomial time on any graph. This contrasts with unweighted graphs, where the number of hunters or firefighters needed can grow linearly in the number of vertices. If the evader, in addition to waiting, may start at an earlier time unknown to the pursuer, we show that the pursuer still wins on any graph given exponential time.

</details>


### [387] [Token-Level Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27382)
*Hanbing Liu,Bowei Zhang,Changyuan Yu,Yinyu Ye,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出LAMA（潜在广告主混合拍卖），一种面向生成式AI的token级广告拍卖机制，将广告主影响力直接嵌入文本生成过程，并证明了其在满足激励相容与个体理性约束的同时逼近最优社会福利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在改变人们获取信息的方式，对围绕预定义广告槽位构建的传统广告机制构成挑战。传统广告依赖于固定位置插槽，而生成式回答是token级自由生成的，亟需一种"生成原生"（generation-native）的广告机制来适配新的信息访问范式。

Method: 提出LAMA机制：广告主上报局部延续价值（local continuation values），诱导出广告主特定的下一token策略；平台通过潜在混合（latent mixture）解码这些策略，并同时更新分配后验。理论上一方面证明LAMA满足Markov DSIC（动态激励相容）与IR（个体理性），并达到接近最优的KL正则化社会福利；另一方面开发了基于学习的实现，从学习到的局部优势（local advantages）和根值（root values）在线重构所需上报。

Result: 在真实商业搜索查询划分上的概念验证实验表明，LAMA在保持面向用户响应质量的同时提升了平台福利与收入，为生成原生广告的可行性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: LAMA将拍卖机制与生成过程深度融合，实现了token级别的广告分配，兼顾激励相容性、社会福利与用户体验，为生成式AI时代的广告生态提供了可行且可学习实现的新范式。

Abstract: Generative AI is transforming how people access information, challenging traditional advertising mechanisms built around predefined slots. Towards generation-native advertising, we propose the Latent Advertiser Mixture Auction (LAMA), a token-level advertising mechanism that embeds advertiser influence directly into the generation process. Advertisers report local continuation values that induce advertiser-specific next-token policies, from which the platform decodes through a latent mixture while updating an allocation posterior. We show that LAMA satisfies Markov DSIC and IR, and achieves near-optimal KL-regularized welfare. We further develop a learning-based implementation that reconstructs the required reports online from learned local advantages and root values. Proof-of-concept experiments on real-world commercial-search query splits show that LAMA improves platform welfare and revenue while maintaining user-facing response quality, providing initial evidence for the feasibility of generation-native advertising.

</details>


### [388] [Algorithms for Robbins' Problem using Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27419)
*Léonard Brice,F. Thomas Bruss,Anirban Majumdar,Jean-François Raskin*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究Robbins问题（秘书选择问题的完全信息变体），其目标是最小化被选中候选人的期望排名。作者将该问题建模为无限马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），提出多种有限状态抽象来近似求解，并发现简单的记忆结构足以获得近优策略。他们首次给出了n≤100范围内所有实例的更好近似值（此前精确值仅知道n≤4，数值近似仅限个位数规模）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robbins问题中，决策者需在面试每位候选人后立即做出去留决定，且不能看到后续候选人、也不能召回已拒绝者，目标是最小化被选者的期望排名。已知最优期望排名最小化需要保留候选人分值完整记忆，这使得问题分析极具挑战；同时现有精确值仅覆盖n≤4，数值近似也只限于个位数规模，缺乏对较大n的良好近似。

Method: 首先将Robbins问题的实例建模为无限马尔可夫决策过程（MDPs）；随后提出若干有限状态抽象来近似这些MDP，从而对固定n逼近问题的值。作者还识别并强调了足以得到近优选择策略的简单记忆结构。

Result: 对于所有5≤n≤100的候选人数，作者给出了比先前已知结果更好的期望排名近似值，填补了此前缺乏良好近似的空白（n>4的精确值此前未知，数值近似仅限不超过一位数的规模）。

Conclusion: 虽然完整的过去候选人分值记忆对最优策略是必要的，但本文表明简单的记忆结构足以获得近优选择策略，并通过有限状态MDP抽象为Robbins问题在较大规模（n≤100）下提供了更好的近似值。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider Robbins' problem, which is a full information variant of the well-known secretary selection problem. In this version of the problem, the goal is to minimize the expected rank of the selected candidate among $n$ that are interviewed sequentially, and a decision to select or not the $m^{th}$ candidate needs to be taken right after the interview (so without seeing the last $n-m$ candidates and without recall). We first show how to model instances of Robbins' problem as infinite Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). Then we propose several finite-state abstractions of these MDPs that allow us to approximate the value of the problem for fixed $n$. While it is known that the full memory of past candidates' values is necessary for optimal expected rank minimization, making the analysis of the problem challenging, we highlight simple memory structures that are sufficient for obtaining near-optimal selection strategies. Additionally, we provide approximate values for Robbins' problem for numbers of candidates $n$ up to 100 for which no good approximations were previously known (the exact value is only known for instances where $n \leq 4$ and numerical approximations were for small values of $n$ not exceeding one digit), for all $n : 5 \leq n \leq 100$, we give better approximation than what was previously known.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [389] [Weight Distributions of Single Parity-Check Product Codes via Character Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26457)
*Makson Miller Alves Ribeiro,Sara D. Cardell*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了二元单校验乘积码的结构与枚举性质：完全确定了 C_{m,n}=SPC(m)⊗SPC(n) 的广义 Hamming 重量谱，对平方乘积码给出了最大码字重量、齐次重量枚举器的对称条件，并借助对偶码与 MacWilliams 恒等式（Walsh–Hadamard 形式）导出了重量枚举器的闭式表达式，进而利用 Krawtchouk 多项式实现无需穷举的完整重量分布计算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对二元单校验乘积码这一重要码类，缺乏对其结构（如广义 Hamming 重量谱、重量分布）的系统刻画；重量分布通常需要穷举全部码字，计算代价高，因此需要理论工具推导闭式公式与高效精确的计算方法。

Method: (1) 以行、列均偶重的 m×n 二元矩阵表示码字；(2) 确定乘积码的广义 Hamming 重量谱；(3) 研究平方乘积码的最大码字重量及齐次重量枚举器的对称性（n 为偶数当且仅当对称）；(4) 刻画对偶码，并利用 MacWilliams 恒等式的 Walsh–Hadamard 形式推导重量枚举器的闭式表达式；(5) 按辅助二元向量的 Hamming 重量分组，得到以二项式系数与交替卷积表示的系数显式公式；(6) 借助 Krawtchouk 多项式给出无需穷举的完整重量分布精确计算流程。

Result: 给出了乘积码 C_{m,n} 的完整广义 Hamming 重量谱；证明了齐次重量枚举器对称当且仅当 n 为偶数；导出了重量枚举器系数的显式闭式公式（由二项式系数和交替卷积构成）；并给出基于 Krawtchouk 多项式的精确重量分布计算算法，数值算例验证了公式与计算结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 本文对二元单校验乘积码的结构与枚举性质给出了完整且精确的理论刻画，提供了无需穷举即可计算完整重量分布的有效工具，为该类码的进一步应用与分析奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate structural and enumerative properties of binary single parity-check product codes. For each $n\geq 2$, $\operatorname{SPC}(n)$ denotes the binary single parity-check code of length $n$, consisting of all binary vectors of length $n$ having even Hamming weight. We determine the generalized Hamming weight hierarchy of the product code $\mathcal{C}_{m,n}=\operatorname{SPC}(m)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, whose codewords can be represented as $m\times n$ binary matrices in which every row and every column has even Hamming weight. For the square product $\mathcal{C}_n =\operatorname{SPC}(n)\otimes\operatorname{SPC}(n)$, we also determine the maximum codeword weight and prove that its homogeneous weight enumerator is symmetric if and only if $n$ is even. After characterizing the dual code, we apply the MacWilliams identity in its Walsh--Hadamard formulation to derive an exact closed-form expression for the weight enumerator. By grouping the auxiliary binary vectors according to their Hamming weights, we obtain an explicit formula for each coefficient in terms of binomial coefficients and alternating convolutions. Finally, using Krawtchouk polynomials, we present an exact procedure for computing the full weight distribution without exhaustively enumerating all codewords. Numerical examples illustrate the formulas and verify the resulting computations.

</details>


### [390] [Decoding Algorithms for MDS Array Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26483)
*Sara D. Cardell,Gustavo Terra Bastos,Cintya Wink de Oliveira Benedito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对由超正则矩阵与非奇异矩阵在有限域上的Kronecker积构造的一类MDS阵列码，利用其校验矩阵的特殊结构，提出了擦除信道下可恢复任意至多n-k个符号擦除的译码算法，以及q元对称信道下定位并校正一个或两个符号错误的显式译码程序，并特别讨论了超正则矩阵为Vandermonde矩阵时的加速译码方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献构造了一类基于超正则矩阵和非奇异矩阵Kronecker积的MDS阵列码，但针对此类码缺乏系统化的译码方法。作者旨在利用其校验矩阵的特殊代数结构，为不同信道模型设计高效、显式的译码算法。

Method: 通过分析该类阵列码校验矩阵的特定结构（超正则矩阵与非奇异矩阵的Kronecker积），分别针对擦除信道设计可恢复任意不超过n-k个符号擦除的译码算法；针对q元对称信道给出一个和两个符号错误的位置与取值判定程序；并在超正则矩阵为Vandermonde矩阵的特殊情形下利用其额外代数结构优化译码过程，辅以不同有限域上的显式算例。

Result: 获得了擦除信道下可恢复任意至多n-k个符号擦除的完整算法，建立了q元对称信道下单错与双错译码的显式位置和取值求解程序，并展示了Vandermonde特殊情形下的结构优势；给出的有限域算例验证了各过程的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用Kronecker积构造的MDS阵列码提供了完整的纠删与纠错译码框架，表明其校验矩阵结构可被有效利用以降低译码复杂度，并为进一步推广到更多错误数及更一般的超正则矩阵情形奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study decoding procedures for a family of MDS array codes previously constructed from the Kronecker product of a superregular matrix and a non-singular matrix over a finite field. By exploiting the particular structure of their parity-check matrices, we develop decoding algorithms for different channel models. For the erasure channel, we provide an algorithm capable of recovering any pattern of up to $n-k$ symbol erasures. For the $q$-ary symmetric channel, we investigate the decoding of one and two symbol errors and give explicit procedures for determining their locations and values. We also consider the particular case in which the superregular matrix is a Vandermonde matrix, showing how its additional algebraic structure can be exploited in the decoding process. Explicit examples over different finite fields are provided to illustrate the proposed procedures.

</details>


### [391] [Sharp Minimax Regret for Infinite-Memory Logistic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26515)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究一类具有无限输入记忆的有限字母表外生驱动信源的在线预测问题，证明其最小最大累计遗憾精确匹配一个“按滞后分辨率谱”Γ_T(r)=Σ_j log(1+n_{T,j}r_j²)，并在指数与多项式衰减包络下给出上下界，从而得到Θ(α⁻¹log²T)与Θ(T^{1/(2s)})的遗憾量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有无限记忆、由外生Rademacher输入驱动的二元信源，现有在线预测理论缺乏精确的最小最大累计遗憾刻画。由于每个滞后j的权重受可加包络r_j约束，且滞后j仅参与n_{T,j}=T-j+1轮预测，该结构使不同滞后对遗憾的贡献截然不同，因此需要按滞后分辨率给出统一的遗憾尺度。

Method: 上层界采用局部化贝叶斯混合（localized Bayesian mixture），证明对任意可和包络有R_T(r)≤CΓ_T(r)；下层界在有限样本维数条件下借助Toeplitz设计（Toeplitz-design）的反向论证给出R_T(r)≥cΓ_T(r)。此外，文中还构造了缩放的在线牛顿预测器（scaled online Newton predictor）以达到谱上界，并讨论了仅保留最近h个输入的截断代价。

Result: 在指数包络r_j=Ae^{-αj}下得到Θ(α⁻¹log²T)，在多项式包络r_j=Aj^{-s}(s>1)下得到Θ(T^{1/(2s)})；截断到最近h个输入的成本量级为Σ_{j>h}n_{T,j}θ_j²，且同一最坏情形截断轮廓可能对应多项式级别不同的遗憾。该反向结论仅适用于外生滞后模型，而非任意平稳无限记忆信源的纯轮廓定理。
